ROOT ExtArmZ.reachable_typed_em0 : ∀ {p : CoreTerm.Prog} {k : Promises.Knobs} {arch : WeakMem.Arch} {Init : TermSound.Res} {c0 : TermRun.Cfg},
  TermMain.ObligationsFX p k arch (fun c c' => TermSpawnStep.Spawn p k arch c0.cap c c' ∨ TermExtStep.Ext p k arch c c')
      Init (fun x => True) c0 →
    k.storeDepRts = true →
      TermMain.InitOK p k arch Init (fun x => True) c0 →
        ∀ {c : TermRun.Cfg}, TermExtStep.StepsE p k arch c0.cap c0 c → ∃ Eg Tot, TermMain.FrameTie p k arch c Eg Tot

CoreLang.Ty.ownHead : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  := fun x =>
  match x with
  | CoreLang.Ty.loc CoreLang.Mode.own τ => true
  | CoreLang.Ty.ptr CoreLang.Mode.own τ => true
  | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt CoreLang.Mode.own τ => true
  | x => false
CoreLang.Ty.payloadFree : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  := fun t =>
  have a := (CoreLang.info CoreLang.Ty.top t).arms;
  a == CoreLang.Arms.optArm || a.wide == 0 && a.ptrs == 0

CoreLang.zeroArmP : (CoreLang.Ty → Bool) → CoreLang.IEnv → CoreLang.KEnv → CoreLang.Ty → Option Bool
  eq_def ∀ (sel : CoreLang.Ty → Bool) (env : CoreLang.IEnv) (ke : CoreLang.KEnv) (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  CoreLang.zeroArmP sel env ke x =
    match x with
    | a.sum b => CoreLang.zeroArmP sel env ke a <|> CoreLang.zeroArmP sel env ke b
    | CoreLang.Ty.void => none
    | t.refine ψ => Option.map (fun x => x && CoreLang.Pred.zeroHolds env ψ) (CoreLang.zeroArmP sel env ke t)
    | t.iall => CoreLang.zeroArmP sel (none :: env) ke t
    | t.iex => CoreLang.zeroArmP sel (some 0 :: env) ke t
    | t.mu => CoreLang.zeroArmP sel env (none :: ke) t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => Option.map (fun x => false) (CoreLang.zeroArmP sel env (none :: ke) t)
    | t => if sel t = true then some (CoreLang.zeroableIn env ke t) else none
CoreLang.zeroableIn : CoreLang.IEnv → CoreLang.KEnv → CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (env : CoreLang.IEnv) (ke : CoreLang.KEnv) (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  CoreLang.zeroableIn env ke x =
    match x with
    | CoreLang.Ty.unit => true
    | CoreLang.Ty.void => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.int => true
    | CoreLang.Ty.intS i => CoreLang.Idx.evalIn env i == some 0
    | CoreLang.Ty.fix sg w => true
    | CoreLang.Ty.lanes sg w => true
    | a.prod b => CoreLang.zeroableIn env ke a && CoreLang.zeroableIn env ke b
    | a.sum b =>
      match (a.sum b).tier with
      | CoreLang.Tier.niche =>
        (CoreLang.zeroArmP CoreLang.Ty.payloadFree env ke a <|> CoreLang.zeroArmP CoreLang.Ty.payloadFree env ke b) ==
          some true
      | CoreLang.Tier.wide =>
        (CoreLang.zeroArmP (fun x => true) env ke a <|> CoreLang.zeroArmP (fun x => true) env ke b) == some true
      | CoreLang.Tier.boxed => false
    | σ.code τ k => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.tid => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.opq o => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => CoreLang.zeroableIn env ke t
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptr μ τ => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt μ τ => true
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc μ (CoreLang.Ty.array i τ) => CoreLang.Idx.evalIn env i == some 0
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc μ τ => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.word r P => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.tok r st => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.word2 r P => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.tok2 r P i0 i1 => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.epair r P => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.tokE r P e => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.share r => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.cap s r => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.tvar k => ke.ask k false fun x => x.zero
    | t.mu => CoreLang.zeroableIn env (none :: ke) t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ τ => false
    | t.iall => CoreLang.zeroableIn (none :: env) ke t
    | t.iex => CoreLang.zeroableIn (some 0 :: env) ke t
    | t.refine ψ => CoreLang.zeroableIn env ke t && CoreLang.Pred.zeroHolds env ψ

TermA4.CkW : CoreTerm.Prog → (Nat → CoreLang.Ty) → CoreTerm.Cx → CoreTerm.Expr → Prop
  := fun p w cx e => TermScope.checkW p cx e = Except.ok () ∧ TermA4.VarsW cx ∧ TermA4.WitA cx w
TermA4.VarsW : CoreTerm.Cx → Prop
  := fun cx => ∀ (v : String × CoreLang.Ty × Bool), v ∈ cx.vars → TermScope.wrapOK v.snd.fst = true
TermA4.WitA : CoreTerm.Cx → (Nat → CoreLang.Ty) → Prop
  := fun cx w => ∀ (k : Nat), k < List.length cx.ke → ¬k ∈ cx.opq → TermA4.argTop (w k) = true
TermA4.argTop : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  := fun t => TermA4.wrapTop t && !TermScope.rcar t
TermA4.wrapTop : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  := fun t => TermA4.wrapTopE [] t
TermA4.wrapTopE : List Bool → CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (env : List Bool) (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  TermA4.wrapTopE env x =
    match x with
    | a.sum b => !TermScope.rcarE env a && !TermScope.rcarE env b && TermA4.wrapTopE env a && TermA4.wrapTopE env b
    | t.iex => !TermScope.rcarE env t && TermA4.wrapTopE env t
    | t.refine ψ => !TermScope.rcarE env t && TermA4.wrapTopE env t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => !TermScope.rcarE (false :: env) t && TermA4.wrapTopE (false :: env) t
    | t.mu => TermA4.wrapTopE (TermScope.rcarE (false :: env) t :: env) t
    | a.prod b => TermA4.wrapTopE env a && TermA4.wrapTopE env b
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => TermA4.wrapTopE env t
    | t.iall => TermA4.wrapTopE env t
    | x => true
TermBoxZ.BoxFrLe : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → Prop
  := fun Eg n => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box), Eg.box l = some B → B.fr ≤ n
TermBoxZ.BoxFrOwn : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
    Eg.box l = some B → ∀ (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[B.own]? = some th → B.fr ≤ th.vs.vWr
TermBoxZ.BoxPV : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
    Eg.box l = some B →
      ∀ (w : TermRun.Word), w ∈ B.pay → ∀ (a : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat), w.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a g → w.view < B.fr
TermBoxZ.BoxSto : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box), Eg.box l = some B → Eg.sto l = none
TermBoxZ.PayOwn : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
    Eg.box l = some B →
      ∀ (w : TermRun.Word),
        w ∈ B.pay →
          ∀ (a : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat),
            w.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a g →
              ∀ (cl : TermSound.Cell), Eg.sto a.label = some cl → Eg.pfr a.label = none → cl.owner = some B.own
TermCertScope.CertWinCNX : CoreTerm.Prog →
  Promises.Knobs →
    WeakMem.Arch → (TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop) → TermSound.Res → (TermSound.Res → Prop) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 =>
  ∀ {c c' : TermRun.Cfg} {Eg : TermCut.GEnv} {Tot HP : Nat → TermSound.Res} {HS : TermSound.Res}
    {cr db : Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res} {i : Nat} {th : TermRun.TTh}
    {r : WeakMem.Memory × List WeakMem.Ts × TermRun.TTh},
    TermMain.TCtxX p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 c c' Eg Tot HP HS cr db i th r →
      ∀ (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
        TermMain.ThSysAt p (TermRun.gOf p k arch c i) c.mem c.pairs th Eg (Tot i) (HP i) HS (cr i) (db i) S →
          ∀ (c1 : TermRun.Cfg),
            TermRun.Solo p k arch i c c1 →
              TermRun.Certified p k arch c1 i →
                TermTyped.NoHoldPubN p k arch (TermCut.Cut Eg i S) (TermCut.Cut Eg i S) i c1
TermCertScope.CertWinPNX : CoreTerm.Prog →
  Promises.Knobs →
    WeakMem.Arch → (TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop) → TermSound.Res → (TermSound.Res → Prop) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 =>
  ∀ {c : TermRun.Cfg} {Eg : TermCut.GEnv} {Tot HP : Nat → TermSound.Res} {HS : TermSound.Res}
    {cr db : Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res} {j : Nat} {th : TermRun.TTh} {a : WeakMem.Address} {v : WeakMem.Val}
    {w : WeakMem.Ts},
    TermMain.PCtxX p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 c Eg Tot HP HS cr db j th a v w →
      (∀ (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
          TermMain.ThSysAt p (TermRun.gOf p k arch c j) c.mem c.pairs th Eg (Tot j) (HP j) HS (cr j) (db j) S →
            ∀ (c1 : TermRun.Cfg),
              TermRun.Solo p k arch j (TermRun.promise c j a v w) c1 →
                TermRun.Certified p k arch c1 j →
                  TermTyped.NoHoldPubN p k arch (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) j c1) ∧
        ∀ (HP' HS' : TermSound.Res) (cr' db' : WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) (S1 : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
          TermMain.ThSysAt p (TermRun.gOf p k arch c j) (Array.push c.mem { loc := a, val := v, view := w }) c.pairs
              (TermMain.thP th (Array.size c.mem))
              (TermMain.EgP Eg (TermPlantF.plantOf c Eg j a v w) (TermPlantF.plantN c Eg j a v w) j (Array.size c.mem))
              (Tot j) HP' HS' cr' db' S1 →
            ∀ (c1 : TermRun.Cfg),
              TermRun.Solo p k arch j (TermRun.promise c j a v w) c1 →
                TermRun.Certified p k arch c1 j →
                  TermTyped.NoHoldPubN p k arch (TermCut.Cut Eg j S1)
                    (TermCut.Cut
                      (TermMain.EgP Eg (TermPlantF.plantOf c Eg j a v w) (TermPlantF.plantN c Eg j a v w) j
                        (Array.size c.mem))
                      j S1)
                    j c1
TermClose.Clo : CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun s => TermClose.tb 0 s = true ∧ ∀ (ρ : Nat → Nat) (W : Nat → CoreLang.Ty) (e f : Nat), TermClose.cl ρ W e f s = s
TermClose.addF : CoreLang.Idx → CoreLang.Idx → CoreLang.Idx
  := fun x x_1 =>
  match x, x_1 with
  | CoreLang.Idx.lit a, CoreLang.Idx.lit b => CoreLang.Idx.lit (a + b)
  | i, j => i.add j
TermClose.cl : (Nat → Nat) → (Nat → CoreLang.Ty) → Nat → Nat → CoreLang.Ty → CoreLang.Ty
  eq_def ∀ (ρ : Nat → Nat) (W : Nat → CoreLang.Ty) (c d : Nat) (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  TermClose.cl ρ W c d x =
    match x with
    | CoreLang.Ty.intS i => CoreLang.Ty.intS (TermClose.clI ρ d i)
    | a.prod b => (TermClose.cl ρ W c d a).prod (TermClose.cl ρ W c d b)
    | a.sum b => (TermClose.cl ρ W c d a).sum (TermClose.cl ρ W c d b)
    | a.code b k => (TermClose.cl ρ W c d a).code (TermClose.cl ρ W c d b) k
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => CoreLang.Ty.array (TermClose.clI ρ d i) (TermClose.cl ρ W c d t)
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptr μ t => CoreLang.Ty.ptr (TermClose.clM ρ d μ) (TermClose.cl ρ W c d t)
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt μ t => CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt (TermClose.clM ρ d μ) (TermClose.cl ρ W c d t)
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc μ t => CoreLang.Ty.loc (TermClose.clM ρ d μ) (TermClose.cl ρ W c d t)
    | CoreLang.Ty.word r P => CoreLang.Ty.word (TermClose.clI ρ d r) P
    | CoreLang.Ty.tok r st =>
      CoreLang.Ty.tok (TermClose.clI ρ d r)
        (Option.map
          (fun x =>
            match x with
            | (P, i) => (P, TermClose.clI ρ d i))
          st)
    | CoreLang.Ty.word2 r P => CoreLang.Ty.word2 (TermClose.clI ρ d r) P
    | CoreLang.Ty.tok2 r P i j => CoreLang.Ty.tok2 (TermClose.clI ρ d r) P (TermClose.clI ρ d i) (TermClose.clI ρ d j)
    | CoreLang.Ty.epair r P => CoreLang.Ty.epair (TermClose.clI ρ d r) P
    | CoreLang.Ty.tokE r P e => CoreLang.Ty.tokE (TermClose.clI ρ d r) P (TermClose.clI ρ d e)
    | CoreLang.Ty.share r => CoreLang.Ty.share (TermClose.clI ρ d r)
    | CoreLang.Ty.cap s r => CoreLang.Ty.cap s (TermClose.clI ρ d r)
    | CoreLang.Ty.tvar k => if k < c then CoreLang.Ty.tvar k else W (k - c)
    | t.mu => (TermClose.cl ρ W (c + 1) d t).mu
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => CoreLang.Ty.tex κ (TermClose.cl ρ W (c + 1) d t)
    | t.iall => (TermClose.cl ρ W c (d + 1) t).iall
    | t.iex => (TermClose.cl ρ W c (d + 1) t).iex
    | t.refine ψ => (TermClose.cl ρ W c d t).refine (TermClose.clP ρ d ψ)
    | t => t
TermClose.clI : (Nat → Nat) → Nat → CoreLang.Idx → CoreLang.Idx
  eq_def ∀ (ρ : Nat → Nat) (d : Nat) (x : CoreLang.Idx),
  TermClose.clI ρ d x =
    match x with
    | CoreLang.Idx.lit n => CoreLang.Idx.lit n
    | CoreLang.Idx.var k => if k < d then CoreLang.Idx.var k else CoreLang.Idx.lit (ρ (k - d))
    | i.add j => TermClose.addF (TermClose.clI ρ d i) (TermClose.clI ρ d j)
    | i.sub j => TermClose.subF (TermClose.clI ρ d i) (TermClose.clI ρ d j)
    | CoreLang.Idx.smul n i => TermClose.smulF n (TermClose.clI ρ d i)
TermClose.clM : (Nat → Nat) → Nat → CoreLang.Mode → CoreLang.Mode
  := fun ρ d x =>
  match x with
  | CoreLang.Mode.rgn r => CoreLang.Mode.rgn (TermClose.clI ρ d r)
  | μ => μ
TermClose.clP : (Nat → Nat) → Nat → CoreLang.Pred → CoreLang.Pred
  eq_def ∀ (ρ : Nat → Nat) (d : Nat) (x : CoreLang.Pred),
  TermClose.clP ρ d x =
    match x with
    | CoreLang.Pred.tt => CoreLang.Pred.tt
    | CoreLang.Pred.le i j => CoreLang.Pred.le (TermClose.clI ρ d i) (TermClose.clI ρ d j)
    | CoreLang.Pred.eq i j => CoreLang.Pred.eq (TermClose.clI ρ d i) (TermClose.clI ρ d j)
    | p.and q => (TermClose.clP ρ d p).and (TermClose.clP ρ d q)
    | CoreLang.Pred.ini i => CoreLang.Pred.ini (TermClose.clI ρ d i)
TermClose.smulF : Nat → CoreLang.Idx → CoreLang.Idx
  := fun n x =>
  match x with
  | CoreLang.Idx.lit a => CoreLang.Idx.lit (n * a)
  | i => CoreLang.Idx.smul n i
TermClose.subF : CoreLang.Idx → CoreLang.Idx → CoreLang.Idx
  := fun x x_1 =>
  match x, x_1 with
  | CoreLang.Idx.lit a, CoreLang.Idx.lit b => CoreLang.Idx.lit (a - b)
  | i, j => i.sub j
TermClose.tb : Nat → CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (c : Nat) (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  TermClose.tb c x =
    match x with
    | CoreLang.Ty.tvar k => decide (k < c)
    | a.prod b => TermClose.tb c a && TermClose.tb c b
    | a.sum b => TermClose.tb c a && TermClose.tb c b
    | a.code b k => TermClose.tb c a && TermClose.tb c b
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => TermClose.tb c t
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptr μ t => TermClose.tb c t
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt μ t => TermClose.tb c t
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc μ t => TermClose.tb c t
    | t.mu => TermClose.tb (c + 1) t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => TermClose.tb (c + 1) t
    | t.iall => TermClose.tb c t
    | t.iex => TermClose.tb c t
    | t.refine ψ => TermClose.tb c t
    | x => true
TermCut.Cut : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop) → TermSound.TEnv
  := fun Eg i S =>
  { prog := Eg.prog, sto := TermCut.cutSto Eg i, tid := i, ini := Eg.ini, rini := Eg.rini, pfr := Eg.pfr, seen := S,
    box := Eg.box, fp := Eg.fpOf i }
TermCut.GBoxNamed : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
    Eg.box l = some B →
      ∀ (w : TermRun.Word),
        w ∈ B.pay →
          ∀ (a : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat),
            w.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a g → (∃ c, Eg.sto a.label = some c) ∨ ∃ B', Eg.box a.label = some B'
TermCut.GEnv : Type
  | TermCut.GEnv.mk : CoreTerm.Prog →
  (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option TermSound.Cell) →
    (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat) →
      (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat) →
        (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts) →
          (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option TermSound.Box) →
            (Nat → List WeakMem.Ts) → (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop) → TermCut.GEnv
TermCut.GEnv.InCut : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun Eg i l => ∃ c, Eg.sto l = some c ∧ (c.owner = some i ∨ Eg.Shared l)
TermCut.GEnv.Shared : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun Eg l => ∃ f, Eg.pfr l = some f
TermCut.GEnv.box : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option TermSound.Box
  := fun self => self.6
TermCut.GEnv.fpOf : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → List WeakMem.Ts
  := fun self => self.7
TermCut.GEnv.ini : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat
  := fun self => self.3
TermCut.GEnv.pfr : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts
  := fun self => self.5
TermCut.GEnv.prog : TermCut.GEnv → CoreTerm.Prog
  := fun self => self.1
TermCut.GEnv.pubd : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun self => self.8
TermCut.GEnv.rini : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat
  := fun self => self.4
TermCut.GEnv.sto : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option TermSound.Cell
  := fun self => self.2
TermCut.GEnv.withFp : TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → List WeakMem.Ts) → TermCut.GEnv
  := fun Eg f =>
  { prog := Eg.prog, sto := Eg.sto, ini := Eg.ini, rini := Eg.rini, pfr := Eg.pfr, box := Eg.box, fpOf := f,
    pubd := Eg.pubd }
TermCut.GStoClo : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell), Eg.sto l = some c → TermClose.Clo c.ty
TermCut.IniWG : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun Eg a =>
  ∀ (c : TermSound.Cell) (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    Eg.sto a.label = some c →
      c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
        TermRun.accs e = some us → a.offset < Eg.ini a.label * TermRun.nWords us
TermCut.IniWrG : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun Eg a =>
  ∀ (c : TermSound.Cell) (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    Eg.sto a.label = some c →
      c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
        TermRun.accs e = some us → a.offset < min (Eg.ini a.label) (Eg.rini a.label) * TermRun.nWords us
TermCut.MemHeldG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun Eg c HS =>
  ∃ L Rl,
    L.Nodup ∧
      (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell), Eg.Shared l → Eg.sto l = some cl → l ∈ L) ∧
        (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), l ∈ L → Eg.Shared l) ∧
          HS = TermSound.Res.sum (List.map Rl L) ∧
            ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel),
              l ∈ L →
                (Eg.sto l = none → Rl l = TermSound.Res.zero) ∧
                  ∀ (cl : TermSound.Cell),
                    Eg.sto l = some cl →
                      ∃ vals,
                        (∀ (o : WeakMem.Offset) (w : TermRun.Word),
                            vals[o]? = some w →
                              TermStep.LatestNP c.mem (TermCut.PromAtG Eg c { label := l, offset := o })
                                { label := l, offset := o } w) ∧
                          TermStep.CellOKR (TermCut.PubE Eg) (Eg.ini l) ((TermCut.PubE Eg).rc l) cl.ty vals (Rl l)
TermCut.MemHeldOn : (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop) → TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun Q E s H =>
  ∃ L Rl,
    L.Nodup ∧
      (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell), Q l → E.sto l = some c → l ∈ L) ∧
        (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), l ∈ L → Q l) ∧
          H = TermSound.Res.sum (List.map Rl L) ∧ ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), l ∈ L → TermStep.HeldEntry E s l (Rl l)
TermCut.MemHeldP : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E s H => TermCut.MemHeldOn (TermStep.Priv E) E s H
TermCut.MemHeldS : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E s H => TermCut.MemHeldOn (fun l => ∃ f, E.pfr l = some f) E s H
TermCut.PredUG : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun Eg a => TermCut.IniWG Eg a ∧ ¬TermCut.IniWrG Eg a
TermCut.PromAtG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → WeakMem.Address → List WeakMem.Ts
  := fun Eg c a =>
  if TermCut.WordUnitG Eg a then []
  else
    if TermCut.PredUG Eg a then List.filter (fun t => decide (TermStep.AboveFW c.mem a t)) (TermCut.allProms c)
    else TermCut.allProms c
TermCut.PromsOK : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
    c.ths[i]? = some th → ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts), t ∈ th.promises ++ Eg.fpOf i ↔ t ∈ TermCut.allProms c
TermCut.PubE : TermCut.GEnv → TermSound.TEnv
  := fun Eg =>
  { prog := Eg.prog, sto := fun l => if Eg.Shared l then Eg.sto l else none, ini := Eg.ini, rini := Eg.rini,
    pfr := Eg.pfr, box := Eg.box }
TermCut.WUOff : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → Prop
  := fun T o =>
  (∃ r P, P.ghost = false ∧ TermSound.AtOff T o (CoreLang.Ty.word r P)) ∨
    (∃ r P, TermSound.AtOff T o (CoreLang.Ty.word2 r P)) ∨
      ∃ r P, 1 ≤ o ∧ TermSound.AtOff T (o - 1) (CoreLang.Ty.word2 r P)
TermCut.WordUnitG : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun Eg a => ∃ c, Eg.sto a.label = some c ∧ TermCut.WUOff c.ty a.offset
TermCut.allProms : TermRun.Cfg → List WeakMem.Ts
  := fun c => List.flatMap (fun x => x.promises) c.ths.toList
TermCut.cutSto : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option TermSound.Cell
  := fun Eg i l => if Eg.InCut i l then Eg.sto l else none
TermCut.noOR : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  TermCut.noOR x =
    match x with
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptr CoreLang.Mode.pub τ => true
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptr μ τ => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt CoreLang.Mode.pub τ => true
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt μ τ => false
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc CoreLang.Mode.pub τ => true
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc μ τ => false
    | a.prod b => TermCut.noOR a && TermCut.noOR b
    | a.sum b => TermCut.noOR a && TermCut.noOR b
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => TermCut.noOR t
    | t.mu => TermCut.noOR t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => TermCut.noOR t
    | t.iall => TermCut.noOR t
    | t.iex => TermCut.noOR t
    | t.refine ψ => TermCut.noOR t
    | x => true
TermExtStep.Ext : CoreTerm.Prog → Promises.Knobs → WeakMem.Arch → TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  | TermExtStep.Ext.mk : ∀ {p : CoreTerm.Prog} {k : Promises.Knobs} {arch : WeakMem.Arch} {c : TermRun.Cfg} (i : Nat) (x : String)
  (n : CoreTerm.ExtName) (a : CoreTerm.At) (e : CoreTerm.Expr) (th : TermRun.TTh) (rt : CoreLang.Ty)
  (ws rs : List TermRun.Word),
  c.ths[i]? = some th →
    th.term = CoreTerm.Expr.let_ x (CoreTerm.Op.ext n a) e →
      (TermRun.ExtNeedsNoProm n th → th.promises = []) →
        TermRun.evalAt th.env a = some (rt, ws) →
          n.Admits i ws c.trace rs →
            TermRun.Certified p k arch (TermRun.extCfg c i th x n e ws rs) i →
              TermExtStep.Ext p k arch c
                (TermRun.wake (TermRun.extCfg c i th x n e ws rs) i (TermRun.relView th.vs) n ws)
TermExtStep.StepsE : CoreTerm.Prog → Promises.Knobs → WeakMem.Arch → Nat → TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun p k arch cap =>
  TermSpawnStep.StepsG p k arch fun c c' => TermSpawnStep.Spawn p k arch cap c c' ∨ TermExtStep.Ext p k arch c c'
TermFul.OneAt : List TermRun.Acc → Nat → Prop
  | TermFul.OneAt.here : ∀ (us : List TermRun.Acc), TermFul.OneAt (TermRun.Acc.one :: us) 0
  | TermFul.OneAt.cons : ∀ (u : TermRun.Acc) {us : List TermRun.Acc} {j : Nat}, TermFul.OneAt us j → TermFul.OneAt (u :: us) (j + u.size)
TermLiveProps.CfgCoh : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c => ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[i]? = some th → TermLiveProps.CohAt c.mem th.vs
TermLiveProps.CohAt : WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.VState → Prop
  := fun mem vs => ∀ (b : WeakMem.Address), vs.coh b = 0 ∨ ∃ m, mem[vs.coh b]? = some m ∧ m.loc = b
TermMain.AllNoProm : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c => ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[i]? = some th → th.promises = []
TermMain.CellOwnerLiveM : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
    Eg.sto l = some cl →
      (∃ t m, c.mem[t]? = some m ∧ m.loc.label = l) ∨ Eg.Shared l →
        (∀ (i : Nat), cl.owner = some i → i < c.ths.size) ∧ (cl.owner = none → Eg.Shared l)
TermMain.EgP : TermCut.GEnv → (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts) → (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat) → Nat → Nat → TermCut.GEnv
  := fun Eg P N j n => (TermMain.plantG Eg P N).withFp (TermStepper.fpProm Eg j n)
TermMain.ExclKey : TermSound.Key → Prop
  := fun x =>
  match x with
  | TermSound.Key.rg n => True
  | TermSound.Key.wd n => True
  | TermSound.Key.own l => True
  | x => False
TermMain.FgnPromClosG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    c.ths[i]? = some th →
      t ∈ th.promises →
        c.mem[t]? = some m →
          ∀ (a' : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat),
            m.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a' g →
              ∀ (cm : TermSound.Cell),
                Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cm →
                  CoreLang.Ty.noOwnIn [] cm.ty = true → TermSound.rgnFree cm.ty = true → TermMain.TgtOK Eg c i a'.label
TermMain.FgnPromCntG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    c.ths[i]? = some th →
      t ∈ th.promises →
        c.mem[t]? = some m →
          ∀ (a' : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat),
            m.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a' g →
              ∀ (cm : TermSound.Cell),
                Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cm →
                  TermPtrRts.NotSnd cm.ty m.loc.offset →
                    TermPtrRts.CntOK cm.ty m.loc.offset (TermMem.mw m) → TermMain.TgtOK Eg c i a'.label
TermMain.FrameTie : CoreTerm.Prog → Promises.Knobs → WeakMem.Arch → TermRun.Cfg → TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun p k arch c Eg Tot =>
  ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
    c.ths[i]? = some th →
      ∃ S ρ η cx Rf Rs,
        TermSound.CE η = TermCut.Cut Eg i S ∧
          TermSound.EnvOK p η ∧
            CoreTyping.Typed p cx th.term ∧
              TermSound.FInvR ρ η th.σs (TermSound.ResumeR p th.stack Rs) cx th.env Rf ∧
                Rf + Rs = Tot i ∧
                  TermStep.MemInv (TermRun.gOf p k arch c i) (TermSound.CE η) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th)
TermMain.InitOK : CoreTerm.Prog → Promises.Knobs → WeakMem.Arch → TermSound.Res → (TermSound.Res → Prop) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun p k arch Init MintOK c0 =>
  (∃ Eg Tot HP HS cr db,
      TermSys.SysInvW p k arch Init MintOK c0 Eg Tot HP HS cr db ∧
        (∃ rc, TermMain.RidOK c0 Tot HP HS cr db rc) ∧
          TermMain.OwnFreshZ c0 Tot HP HS cr ∧
            TermUndefZ.UndefAtG Eg c0.mem ∧
              (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                  c0.ths[i]? = some th → TermOwnCohZ.OwnCohZ (TermCut.Cut Eg i S) (TermRun.stOf c0.mem c0.pairs th)) ∧
                (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), Eg.box l = none) ∧
                  (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), Eg.pfr l = none) ∧
                    TermMain.OwnKeyOwnG c0 Eg Tot ∧
                      TermMain.OwnPubG c0 Eg Tot HP HS ∧
                        TermMain.StoNI Eg ∧
                          (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
                              Eg.sto l = some cl → TermA4.wrapTop cl.ty = true) ∧
                            TermPtrRts.LblSto Eg c0 ∧
                              (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
                                  Eg.sto l = some cl → TermSound.LayT cl.ty) ∧
                                c0.mem = #[] ∧
                                  TermViewInv.ViewInv c0 ∧
                                    (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
                                        c0.ths[i]? = some th → th.vs.vWr ≤ Array.size c0.mem - 1) ∧
                                      ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c0.ths[i]? = some th → th.tidLbl = none) ∧
    TermMain.AllNoProm c0 ∧ TermLiveProps.CfgCoh c0
TermMain.KeyNet : TermRun.Cfg →
  (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
    (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
      TermSound.Res → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Key → Prop
  := fun c Tot HP HS cr db k =>
  (List.map (fun i => (Tot i + HP i + TermSys.pSumAt c i (cr i)) k) (List.range c.ths.size)).sum + HS k ≤
    1 + (List.map (fun i => TermSys.pSumAt c i (db i) k) (List.range c.ths.size)).sum
TermMain.MsgWordsG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
    c.mem[t]? = some m → Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cl → TermStep.InWords cl.ty m.loc.offset
TermMain.ObligationsFX : CoreTerm.Prog →
  Promises.Knobs →
    WeakMem.Arch → (TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop) → TermSound.Res → (TermSound.Res → Prop) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  | TermMain.ObligationsFX.mk : ∀ {p : CoreTerm.Prog} {k : Promises.Knobs} {arch : WeakMem.Arch} {Sp : TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop}
  {Init : TermSound.Res} {MintOK : TermSound.Res → Prop} {c0 : TermRun.Cfg},
  CoreTerm.checkProg p = Except.ok () →
    TermScope.ProgScopeOKW p = true →
      TermScope.RunScope c0 →
        TermCertScope.CertWinCNX p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 →
          TermCertScope.CertWinPNX p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 → TermMain.ObligationsFX p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0
TermMain.OwnFreshZ : TermRun.Cfg → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun c Tot HP HS cr =>
  ∀ (q : Nat) (thq : TermRun.TTh) (j : Nat),
    c.ths[q]? = some thq →
      thq.nalloc ≤ j →
        (∀ (n : Nat),
            n < c.ths.size →
              (Tot n + HP n + TermSys.pSumAt c n (cr n)) (TermSound.Key.own (TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase c.n q j)) = 0) ∧
          HS (TermSound.Key.own (TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase c.n q j)) = 0
TermMain.OwnKeyOwnG : TermRun.Cfg → TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun c Eg Tot =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      Tot j (TermSound.Key.own l) ≠ 0 → Eg.sto l = none ∨ ∃ cl, Eg.sto l = some cl ∧ cl.owner = some j
TermMain.OwnPubG : TermRun.Cfg → TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun c Eg Tot HP HS =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel),
    Eg.pubd l →
      Eg.pfr l ≠ none ∧
        (Eg.sto l ≠ none →
          (∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[j]? = some th → (Tot j + HP j) (TermSound.Key.own l) = 0) ∧
            HS (TermSound.Key.own l) = 0)
TermMain.PCtxX : CoreTerm.Prog →
  Promises.Knobs →
    WeakMem.Arch →
      (TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop) →
        TermSound.Res →
          (TermSound.Res → Prop) →
            TermRun.Cfg →
              TermRun.Cfg →
                TermCut.GEnv →
                  (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                    (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                      TermSound.Res →
                        (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) →
                          (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) →
                            Nat → TermRun.TTh → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Val → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  | TermMain.PCtxX.mk : ∀ {p : CoreTerm.Prog} {k : Promises.Knobs} {arch : WeakMem.Arch} {Sp : TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop}
  {Init : TermSound.Res} {MintOK : TermSound.Res → Prop} {c0 c : TermRun.Cfg} {Eg : TermCut.GEnv}
  {Tot HP : Nat → TermSound.Res} {HS : TermSound.Res} {cr db : Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res} {j : Nat}
  {th : TermRun.TTh} {a : WeakMem.Address} {v : WeakMem.Val} {w : WeakMem.Ts},
  TermSpawnStep.StepsG p k arch Sp c0 c →
    TermMain.SysInvN p k arch Init MintOK c Eg Tot HP HS cr db →
      c.ths[j]? = some th →
        w < Array.size c.mem →
          TermRun.Certified p k arch (TermRun.promise c j a v w) j →
            TermMain.PCtxX p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 c Eg Tot HP HS cr db j th a v w
TermMain.PromClosG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      t ∈ th.promises →
        c.mem[t]? = some m →
          ∀ (a' : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat),
            m.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a' g →
              ∀ (cl : TermSound.Cell),
                Eg.sto a'.label = some cl →
                  cl.owner = some j →
                    ∀ (cm : TermSound.Cell),
                      Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cm →
                        cm.owner = some j ∨ Eg.Shared m.loc.label →
                          CoreLang.Ty.noOwnIn [] cm.ty = true →
                            TermSound.rgnFree cm.ty = true → ∃ f, Eg.pfr a'.label = some f
TermMain.PromIniOK : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      t ∈ th.promises →
        c.mem[t]? = some m →
          ∀ (cl : TermSound.Cell),
            Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cl →
              Eg.Shared m.loc.label → TermMem.InitR (TermCut.PubE Eg) cl m.loc.label m.loc.offset
TermMain.PromNoTwoOK : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      t ∈ th.promises →
        c.mem[t]? = some m →
          Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cl → ∀ (o : Nat), TermStep.TwoAtT cl.ty o → m.loc.offset ≠ o ∧ m.loc.offset ≠ o + 1
TermMain.PromNotUndef : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c =>
  ∀ (q : Nat) (thq : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    c.ths[q]? = some thq → t ∈ thq.promises → c.mem[t]? = some m → m.val ≠ WeakMem.Val.undef
TermMain.PromTyAOK : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      t ∈ th.promises →
        c.mem[t]? = some m →
          ∀ (cl : TermSound.Cell),
            Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cl →
              Eg.Shared m.loc.label → TermMem.WordOK (TermCut.PubE Eg) cl.ty m.loc.offset (TermMem.mw m)
TermMain.RidOK : TermRun.Cfg →
  (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
    (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
      TermSound.Res → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → Nat) → Prop
  := fun c Tot HP HS cr db rc =>
  ∀ (n : Nat),
    ¬TermOnce.RidUsed rc n →
      ∀ (key : TermSound.Key),
        key = TermSound.Key.rg n ∨ key = TermSound.Key.wd n →
          (List.map (fun i => (Tot i + HP i + TermSys.pSumAt c i (cr i)) key) (List.range c.ths.size)).sum + HS key ≤
            (List.map (fun i => TermSys.pSumAt c i (db i) key) (List.range c.ths.size)).sum
TermMain.StoNI : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell), Eg.sto l = some cl → TermTv.noIallV cl.ty = true
TermMain.SysInvN : CoreTerm.Prog →
  Promises.Knobs →
    WeakMem.Arch →
      TermSound.Res →
        (TermSound.Res → Prop) →
          TermRun.Cfg →
            TermCut.GEnv →
              (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                  TermSound.Res → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  | TermMain.SysInvN.mk : ∀ {p : CoreTerm.Prog} {k : Promises.Knobs} {arch : WeakMem.Arch} {Init : TermSound.Res} {MintOK : TermSound.Res → Prop}
  {c : TermRun.Cfg} {Eg : TermCut.GEnv} {Tot HP : Nat → TermSound.Res} {HS : TermSound.Res}
  {cr db : Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res},
  (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
      c.ths[i]? = some th →
        TermSys.ThSys p (TermRun.gOf p k arch c i) c.mem c.pairs th Eg (Tot i) (HP i) HS (cr i) (db i)) →
    TermCut.MemHeldG Eg c HS →
      TermCut.GStoClo Eg →
        TermSys.GStoMg Eg →
          TermSys.SharedTyOK Eg →
            TermCut.PromsOK Eg c →
              (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                  c.ths[i]? = some th → TermStep.FpOK (TermCut.Cut Eg i S) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th)) →
                (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
                    c.ths[i]? = some th →
                      ∀ (j : Nat), th.nalloc ≤ j → TermSys.GFreshL Eg c th (TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase c.n i j)) →
                  (∃ M,
                      MintOK M ∧
                        (TermSys.thSum c.ths.size fun i => Tot i + TermSys.pSumAt c i (cr i) + HP i) + HS ≤
                          Init + M + TermSys.thSum c.ths.size fun i => TermSys.pSumAt c i (db i)) →
                    (∀ (key : TermSound.Key), TermMain.ExclKey key → TermMain.KeyNet c Tot HP HS cr db key) →
                      c.boxFence = true →
                        TermSys.BoxTopG Eg c →
                          TermSys.PromsDisj c →
                            TermSys.FpOwnFree Eg c →
                              TermSys.AllPromLt c →
                                TermSys.VWrLe c →
                                  TermSys.FrontierLe Eg (Array.size c.mem) →
                                    (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), Eg.rini l ≤ Eg.ini l) →
                                      TermZwIni.ZwIniG Eg →
                                        TermPredHold.PredHoldG Eg c →
                                          TermCut.GBoxNamed Eg →
                                            (∃ rc, TermMain.RidOK c Tot HP HS cr db rc) →
                                              TermMain.PromIniOK Eg c →
                                                TermMain.PromTyAOK Eg c →
                                                  TermMain.PromNotUndef c →
                                                    TermMain.OwnFreshZ c Tot HP HS cr →
                                                      TermMain.PromNoTwoOK Eg c →
                                                        TermUndefZ.UndefAtG Eg c.mem →
                                                          TermMain.OwnKeyOwnG c Eg Tot →
                                                            TermMain.OwnPubG c Eg Tot HP HS →
                                                              TermBoxZ.BoxPV Eg →
                                                                TermBoxZ.BoxFrOwn Eg c →
                                                                  TermBoxZ.BoxFrLe Eg (Array.size c.mem) →
                                                                    (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
                                                                        c.ths[i]? = some th →
                                                                          th.vs.vWr ≤ Array.size c.mem - 1) →
                                                                      TermBoxZ.BoxFrLe Eg (Array.size c.mem - 1) →
                                                                        TermSys.FrontierLe Eg (Array.size c.mem - 1) →
                                                                          TermBoxZ.PayOwn Eg →
                                                                            TermBoxZ.BoxSto Eg →
                                                                              (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh)
                                                                                  (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                                                                                  c.ths[i]? = some th →
                                                                                    TermOwnCohZ.OwnCohZ
                                                                                      (TermCut.Cut Eg i S)
                                                                                      (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th)) →
                                                                                (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh)
                                                                                    (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                                                                                    c.ths[i]? = some th →
                                                                                      TermTyped.RiniCoh
                                                                                        (TermCut.Cut Eg i S)
                                                                                        (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs

                                                                                      TermMain.StoNI Eg →

                                                                                            TermMain.MsgWordsG Eg c →

TermMain.TCtxX : CoreTerm.Prog →
  Promises.Knobs →
    WeakMem.Arch →
      (TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop) →
        TermSound.Res →
          (TermSound.Res → Prop) →
            TermRun.Cfg →
              TermRun.Cfg →
                TermRun.Cfg →
                  TermCut.GEnv →
                    (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                      (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                        TermSound.Res →
                          (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) →
                            (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) →
                              Nat → TermRun.TTh → WeakMem.Memory × List WeakMem.Ts × TermRun.TTh → Prop
  | TermMain.TCtxX.mk : ∀ {p : CoreTerm.Prog} {k : Promises.Knobs} {arch : WeakMem.Arch} {Sp : TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.Cfg → Prop}
  {Init : TermSound.Res} {MintOK : TermSound.Res → Prop} {c0 c c' : TermRun.Cfg} {Eg : TermCut.GEnv}
  {Tot HP : Nat → TermSound.Res} {HS : TermSound.Res} {cr db : Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res} {i : Nat}
  {th : TermRun.TTh} {r : WeakMem.Memory × List WeakMem.Ts × TermRun.TTh},
  TermSpawnStep.StepsG p k arch Sp c0 c →
    TermMain.SysInvN p k arch Init MintOK c Eg Tot HP HS cr db →
      c.ths[i]? = some th →
        r ∈ TermRun.tstep (TermRun.gOf p k arch c i) c.mem c.pairs th →
          c' ∈ TermRun.threadSteps p k arch c i →
            c'.mem = r.fst →
              c'.pairs = r.snd.fst →
                c'.ths[i]? = some r.snd.snd →
                  (∀ (j : Nat), j ≠ i → c'.ths[j]? = c.ths[j]?) →
                    TermRun.Certified p k arch c' i →
                      TermMain.TCtxX p k arch Sp Init MintOK c0 c c' Eg Tot HP HS cr db i th r
TermMain.TgtOK : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Nat → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun Eg c i l =>
  (∃ f, Eg.pfr l = some f) ∨
    (∃ cl, Eg.sto l = some cl ∧ cl.owner = some i) ∨
      (∃ B, Eg.box l = some B) ∨ ∃ n, l = TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase c.n i n ∧ Eg.sto l = none
TermMain.ThSysAt : CoreTerm.Prog →
  TermRun.G →
    WeakMem.Memory →
      List WeakMem.Ts →
        TermRun.TTh →
          TermCut.GEnv →
            TermSound.Res →
              TermSound.Res →
                TermSound.Res →
                  (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop) → Prop
  := fun p g mem pairs th Eg Tot HP HS cr db S =>
  TermSound.ThInvRE p (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) th Tot ∧
    TermSound.ThInvE p (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) th ∧
      TermStep.MemInv g (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) ∧
        TermCut.MemHeldP (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) HP ∧
          TermCut.MemHeldS (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) HS ∧
            TermPlant.PromCrK (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) cr db
TermMain.TidBox : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.TTh → Prop
  := fun Eg th =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel),
    th.tidLbl = some l → Eg.sto l = some { ty := CoreLang.Ty.unit, owner := none } ∧ Eg.pfr l = some 0
TermMain.plantG : TermCut.GEnv → (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts) → (WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat) → TermCut.GEnv
  := fun Eg P N =>
  { prog := Eg.prog, sto := Eg.sto,
    ini := fun l =>
      match P l with
      | some val => max (Eg.ini l) (N l)
      | none => Eg.ini l,
    rini := Eg.rini,
    pfr := fun l =>
      match P l with
      | some f => some f
      | none => Eg.pfr l,
    box := Eg.box, fpOf := Eg.fpOf, pubd := Eg.pubd }
TermMain.thP : TermRun.TTh → Nat → TermRun.TTh
  := fun th n =>
  { term := th.term, env := th.env, σs := th.σs, stack := th.stack, vs := th.vs, promises := th.promises ++ [n],
    nalloc := th.nalloc, undefs := th.undefs, tidLbl := th.tidLbl, permit := th.permit }
TermMem.BufOK : TermSound.TEnv → Nat → CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun η n T vals =>
  ∃ N e us,
    T = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
      TermRun.accs e = some us ∧
        n ≤ N ∧
          vals.length = N * TermRun.nWords us ∧
            ∀ (j : Nat),
              j < n → TermMem.ValOf η e (List.take (TermRun.nWords us) (List.drop (j * TermRun.nWords us) vals))
TermMem.CellOK : TermSound.TEnv → Nat → CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun η n T vals => TermSound.wc T = some vals.length ∧ TermMem.ValOf η T vals ∨ TermMem.BufOK η n T vals
TermMem.InitR : TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.Cell → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → Prop
  := fun η c l o =>
  ∀ (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e → TermRun.accs e = some us → o < η.ini l * TermRun.nWords us
TermMem.PairOK : TermSound.TEnv → CoreLang.Ty → Nat → TermRun.Word → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun η T o w0 w1 =>
  ∃ b S ws o' x0 x1,
    TermMem.SegOf T b S ∧
      TermMem.ValOfS η S ws ∧
        ws[o']? = some x0 ∧ ws[o' + 1]? = some x1 ∧ TermSound.WLe1 x0 w0 ∧ TermSound.WLe1 x1 w1 ∧ b + o' = o
TermMem.SeenLE : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun η V => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), η.seen l → ∃ f, η.pfr l = some f ∧ f ≤ V
TermMem.SegOf : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun T b S =>
  b = 0 ∧ S = T ∧ TermRun.accs T ≠ none ∨
    ∃ N us j,
      T = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) S ∧ TermRun.accs S = some us ∧ j < N ∧ b = j * TermRun.nWords us
TermMem.ValOf : TermSound.TEnv → CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun η T ws => TermSound.VR (fun x => 0) (TermSound.CE η.unsee) T (TermSound.shp T) ws ∧ TermSound.Valid T ws
TermMem.ValOfS : TermSound.TEnv → CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun η S ws => TermMem.ValOf η S ws ∧ TermSound.ShapeC S ws
TermMem.WordOK : TermSound.TEnv → CoreLang.Ty → Nat → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun η T o w =>
  ∃ b S ws o' w0, TermMem.SegOf T b S ∧ TermMem.ValOfS η S ws ∧ ws[o']? = some w0 ∧ TermSound.WLe1 w0 w ∧ b + o' = o
TermMem.mw : WeakMem.Msg → TermRun.Word
  := fun m => { val := m.val, view := m.view, rts := 0 }
TermOnce.RidUsed : (Nat → Nat) → Nat → Prop
  := fun rc n => ∃ t k, k < rc t ∧ n = TermStep.ridPair t k
TermOverCr.CovU : TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.Cell → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → Prop
  := fun E c l o => TermStep.InitRr E c l o ∨ TermStep.PredU E { label := l, offset := o }
TermOverCr.CrItemQ : TermSound.TEnv →
  TermRun.St →
    (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) →
      WeakMem.AllocLabel →
        TermSound.Cell → WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool) → Prop
  := fun E s cr l c x =>
  ∃ mh usS,
    s.mem[x.fst]? = some mh ∧
      mh.loc.label = l ∧
        TermPiece.LowOwn s mh.loc x.fst ∧
          ¬TermStep.WordUnit E mh.loc ∧
            TermSound.AtOff c.ty x.snd.fst x.snd.snd.fst ∧
              TermStep.Solid x.snd.snd.fst ∧
                TermRun.accs x.snd.snd.fst = some usS ∧
                  x.snd.fst ≤ mh.loc.offset ∧
                    mh.loc.offset < x.snd.fst + TermRun.nWords usS ∧
                      x.snd.snd.snd.fst.length = TermRun.nWords usS ∧
                        TermStep.ValOfR E x.snd.snd.fst x.snd.snd.snd.fst x.snd.snd.snd.snd.fst ∧
                          x.snd.snd.snd.snd.fst ≤ cr x.fst ∧
                            ∀ (i o : Nat) (w : TermRun.Word) (tw' : WeakMem.Ts),
                              x.snd.snd.snd.snd.snd i = true →
                                TermStep.InAcc usS i o →
                                  x.snd.snd.snd.fst[o]? = some w →
                                    TermPiece.LowOwn s { label := l, offset := x.snd.fst + o } tw' →
                                      TermStep.PredAt s.mem { label := l, offset := x.snd.fst + o } tw' w ∨
                                        TermStep.PredU E { label := l, offset := x.snd.fst + o } ∧
                                          TermStep.FirstW s.mem { label := l, offset := x.snd.fst + o } tw' ∧
                                            ∃ m, s.mem[tw']? = some m ∧ TermMem.mw m = w
TermOverCr.HolderLow : TermRun.St →
  WeakMem.AllocLabel → List (WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool)) → Prop
  := fun s l Gs =>
  ∀ (x : WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool)),
    x ∈ Gs →
      ∀ (usS : List TermRun.Acc),
        TermRun.accs x.snd.snd.fst = some usS →
          ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
            t ∈ s.promises →
              s.mem[t]? = some m →
                m.loc.label = l → x.snd.fst ≤ m.loc.offset → m.loc.offset < x.snd.fst + TermRun.nWords usS → x.fst ≤ t
TermOverCr.OverCrE : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → WeakMem.AllocLabel → TermSound.Cell → Prop
  := fun E s cr l c => ∃ Gs, TermOverCr.OverCrL E s cr l c (TermOverCr.WinG E s l) Gs ∧ TermOverCr.HolderLow s l Gs
TermOverCr.OverCrG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun Eg c cr =>
  ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
    c.ths[i]? = some th →
      ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
        (TermCut.Cut Eg i S).sto l = some cl →
          cl.owner = some i →
            Eg.pfr l ≠ none → TermOverCr.OverCrE (TermCut.Cut Eg i S) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th) (cr i) l cl
TermOverCr.OverCrL : TermSound.TEnv →
  TermRun.St →
    (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) →
      WeakMem.AllocLabel →
        TermSound.Cell →
          (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → Prop) →
            List (WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool)) → Prop
  := fun E s cr l c G Gs =>
  (List.map (fun x => x.fst) Gs).Nodup ∧
    (∀ (x : WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool)),
        x ∈ Gs → TermOverCr.CrItemQ E s cr l c x) ∧
      (∀ (o : Nat) (tw : WeakMem.Ts),
          TermOverCr.CovU E c l o →
            ¬TermStep.VisU E { label := l, offset := o } →
              TermPiece.LowOwn s { label := l, offset := o } tw →
                G o tw →
                  TermPiece.OverT E s { label := l, offset := o } tw →
                    ∃ x,
                      x ∈ Gs ∧
                        ∃ usS i,
                          TermRun.accs x.snd.snd.fst = some usS ∧
                            x.snd.fst ≤ o ∧ TermStep.InAcc usS i (o - x.snd.fst) ∧ x.snd.snd.snd.snd.snd i = true) ∧
        (∀ (x : WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool)),
            x ∈ Gs →
              ∀ (usS : List TermRun.Acc) (i o : Nat),
                TermRun.accs x.snd.snd.fst = some usS →
                  x.snd.snd.snd.snd.snd i = true →
                    TermStep.InAcc usS i o → ∃ t0, TermPiece.LowOwn s { label := l, offset := x.snd.fst + o } t0) ∧
          ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
            t ∈ s.promises →
              s.mem[t]? = some m →
                m.loc.label = l →
                  ¬TermStep.WordUnit E m.loc → cr t ≠ TermSound.Res.zero → t ∈ List.map (fun x => x.fst) Gs
TermOverCr.PromAboveG : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c => ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[j]? = some th → TermStep.PromAbove (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th)
TermOverCr.WinG : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun E s l o tw =>
  (∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
      s.mem[t]? = some m → m.loc = { label := l, offset := o } → t ≤ tw → t ∈ s.promises ∨ t ≤ s.vs.coh m.loc) ∧
    ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
      t ∈ TermStep.FpAt E { label := l, offset := 0 } →
        s.mem[t]? = some m → m.loc = { label := l, offset := o } → tw < t
TermOwnCohZ.OwnCohZ : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun E s =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell),
    E.sto l = some c →
      c.owner = some E.tid →
        E.pfr l = none →
          ∀ (o : Nat),
            (∃ E0 n vals, TermMem.CellOK E0 n c.ty vals ∧ o < vals.length) →
              s.vs.coh { label := l, offset := o } ≤ s.vs.vWr
TermPiece.CrItem : TermSound.TEnv →
  TermRun.St →
    (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) →
      WeakMem.AllocLabel →
        TermSound.Cell → WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool) → Prop
  := fun E s cr l c x =>
  ∃ mh usS,
    s.mem[x.fst]? = some mh ∧
      mh.loc.label = l ∧
        TermPiece.LowOwn s mh.loc x.fst ∧
          ¬TermStep.WordUnit E mh.loc ∧
            TermSound.AtOff c.ty x.snd.fst x.snd.snd.fst ∧
              TermStep.Solid x.snd.snd.fst ∧
                TermRun.accs x.snd.snd.fst = some usS ∧
                  x.snd.fst ≤ mh.loc.offset ∧
                    mh.loc.offset < x.snd.fst + TermRun.nWords usS ∧
                      x.snd.snd.snd.fst.length = TermRun.nWords usS ∧
                        TermStep.ValOfR E x.snd.snd.fst x.snd.snd.snd.fst x.snd.snd.snd.snd.fst ∧
                          x.snd.snd.snd.snd.fst ≤ cr x.fst ∧
                            ∀ (i o : Nat) (w : TermRun.Word) (tw' : WeakMem.Ts),
                              x.snd.snd.snd.snd.snd i = true →
                                TermStep.InAcc usS i o →
                                  x.snd.snd.snd.fst[o]? = some w →
                                    TermPiece.LowOwn s { label := l, offset := x.snd.fst + o } tw' →
                                      TermStep.PredAt s.mem { label := l, offset := x.snd.fst + o } tw' w
TermPiece.LowOwn : TermRun.St → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun s a tw =>
  tw ∈ s.promises ∧
    (∃ m, s.mem[tw]? = some m ∧ m.loc = a) ∧
      ∀ (t' : WeakMem.Ts) (m' : WeakMem.Msg), t' ∈ s.promises → s.mem[t']? = some m' → m'.loc = a → tw ≤ t'
TermPiece.OverT : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun E s a tw => ∃ t m, tw < t ∧ s.mem[t]? = some m ∧ m.loc = a ∧ ¬t ∈ TermStep.PromAt E s a
TermPlant.CrKeys : TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun R => ∀ (l : Nat), R (TermSound.Key.own l) = 0
TermPlant.PromCrK : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun E s cr db =>
  ∀ (tw : WeakMem.Ts),
    tw ∈ s.promises →
      ∃ m,
        s.mem[tw]? = some m ∧
          TermStep.DbRes E s.mem m tw (db tw) ∧
            (TermStep.OldCr E s m tw (cr tw) ∨ TermStep.PlantCr E s m tw (cr tw) ∧ TermPlant.CrKeys (cr tw))
TermPlantAtT.KeyG : (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → Nat → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun Tot HP j l => 0 < (Tot j + HP j) (TermSound.Key.own l)
TermPlantAtT.PlantIdxG : (TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Nat → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts) →
  TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun tm Eg Tot HP c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      Eg.sto l = some cl →
        cl.owner = some j →
          Eg.pfr l ≠ none →
            TermPlantAtT.KeyG Tot HP j l →
              ∃ t mP,
                tm Eg c j l = some t ∧
                  c.mem[t]? = some mP ∧
                    t ∈ th.promises ∧
                      th.vs.vWr ≤ mP.view ∧
                        (∀ (u : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
                            c.mem[u]? = some m → m.loc.label = l → u ≤ t → u ∈ th.promises ∨ u ≤ th.vs.coh m.loc) ∧
                          ∀ (u : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
                            u ∈ Eg.fpOf j → c.mem[u]? = some m → m.loc.label = l → t < u
TermPlantF.PlantL : TermRun.Cfg → TermCut.GEnv → Nat → TermRun.TTh → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Val → WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun c Eg j th a v w l =>
  ∃ l',
    TermStep.ClR
        (TermTyped.PlantR (TermPlantF.cutP Eg j) (Array.push c.mem { loc := a, val := v, view := w })
          (th.promises ++ [Array.size c.mem]))
        a.label l' ∧
      TermTyped.PlantR (TermPlantF.cutP Eg j) (Array.push c.mem { loc := a, val := v, view := w })
        (th.promises ++ [Array.size c.mem]) l' l
TermPlantF.cutP : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → TermSound.TEnv
  := fun Eg j => TermCut.Cut Eg j fun x => False
TermPlantF.plantN : TermRun.Cfg → TermCut.GEnv → Nat → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Val → WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat
  := fun c Eg j a v w l =>
  match c.ths[j]? with
  | none => 0
  | some th => TermStep.NpZ (TermPlantF.cutP Eg j) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th) l
TermPlantF.plantOf : TermRun.Cfg → TermCut.GEnv → Nat → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Val → WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts
  := fun c Eg j a v w l =>
  match c.ths[j]? with
  | none => none
  | some th =>
    if TermPlantF.PlantL c Eg j th a v w l then
      some (TermStep.plantF (TermPlantF.cutP Eg j) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th) l)
    else none
TermPool.PieceLow : WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → CoreLang.Ty → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun mem P l b S t =>
  ∀ (usS : List TermRun.Acc),
    TermRun.accs S = some usS →
      ∀ (t' : WeakMem.Ts) (m' : WeakMem.Msg),
        t' ∈ P →
          mem[t']? = some m' → m'.loc.label = l → b ≤ m'.loc.offset → m'.loc.offset < b + TermRun.nWords usS → t ≤ t'
TermPool.PoolCovUL : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun Eg c pl =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (thj : TermRun.TTh) (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
    c.ths[j]? = some thj →
      ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
        (TermCut.Cut Eg j S).sto l = some cl →
          cl.owner = some j →
            Eg.pfr l ≠ none →
              ¬Eg.pubd l →
                ∃ Gs,
                  (∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts),
                      TermSound.Res.sum
                          (List.map (fun x => x.snd.snd.snd.snd.fst) (List.filter (fun x => decide (x.fst = t)) Gs)) ≤
                        pl j t) ∧
                    (∀ (x : WeakMem.Ts × Nat × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word × TermSound.Res × (Nat → Bool)),
                        x ∈ Gs →
                          TermOverCr.CrItemQ (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs thj) (pl j) l cl x) ∧
                      ∀ (o : WeakMem.Offset) (tw : WeakMem.Ts),
                        TermStep.PredU (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) { label := l, offset := o } →
                          ¬TermStep.VisU (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) { label := l, offset := o } →
                            TermPiece.LowOwn (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs thj) { label := l, offset := o } tw →
                              TermStep.FirstW c.mem { label := l, offset := o } tw →
                                TermPiece.OverT (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs thj)
                                    { label := l, offset := o } tw →
                                  ∃ x,
                                    x ∈ Gs ∧
                                      ∃ usS i,
                                        TermRun.accs x.snd.snd.fst = some usS ∧
                                          x.snd.fst ≤ o ∧
                                            TermStep.InAcc usS i (o - x.snd.fst) ∧ x.snd.snd.snd.snd.snd i = true
TermPool.PoolTag : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun Eg c pl =>
  (∀ (j : Nat) (thj : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts),
      c.ths[j]? = some thj →
        pl j t ≠ TermSound.Res.zero →
          t ∈ thj.promises ∧
            TermPlant.CrKeys (pl j t) ∧
              ∃ m,
                c.mem[t]? = some m ∧
                  ((∀ (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                        ∃ f mf,
                          f ∈ TermStep.FpAt (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) { label := m.loc.label, offset := 0 } ∧
                            c.mem[f]? = some mf ∧ mf.loc.label = m.loc.label ∧ f ≤ t) ∨
                      ∀ (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                        (TermCut.Cut Eg j S).pfr m.loc.label ≠ none ∧
                          ∃ cl x,
                            (TermCut.Cut Eg j S).sto m.loc.label = some cl ∧
                              cl.owner = some j ∧
                                x.fst = t ∧
                                  (∀ (i : Nat), x.snd.snd.snd.snd.snd i = false) ∧
                                    TermPiece.CrItem (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs thj) (pl j)
                                      m.loc.label cl x) ∧
                    ∀ (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                      ∃ cl x,
                        (TermCut.Cut Eg j S).sto m.loc.label = some cl ∧
                          cl.owner = some j ∧
                            x.fst = t ∧
                              TermPiece.CrItem (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs thj) (pl j) m.loc.label
                                  cl x ∧
                                TermPool.PieceLow c.mem thj.promises m.loc.label x.snd.fst x.snd.snd.fst t) ∧
    TermPool.PoolCovUL Eg c pl
TermPool.crPl : (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res
  := fun cr pl i t => cr i t + pl i t
TermPredHold.PredHoldG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop) (a : WeakMem.Address),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      (∃ cl, Eg.sto a.label = some cl ∧ cl.owner = some j) →
        ¬Eg.pubd a.label →
          TermStep.PredU (TermCut.Cut Eg j S) a →
            ∃ h, TermStep.FirstW c.mem a h ∧ h ∈ th.promises ∧ ∀ (F : WeakMem.Ts), Eg.pfr a.label = some F → h ≤ F
TermPtrRts.BoxPlantSep : TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg Tot HP c =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
    Eg.box l = some B →
      ∀ (o : WeakMem.Offset) (w : TermRun.Word),
        B.pay[o]? = some w →
          ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (a : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell) (f t : WeakMem.Ts),
            c.ths[j]? = some th →
              Eg.sto a = some cl →
                cl.owner = some j →
                  Eg.pfr a = some f →
                    0 < (Tot j + HP j) (TermSound.Key.own a) →
                      TermPtrRts.tminG Eg c j a = some t →
                        TermPtrRts.Names a w →
                          w.view < f ∨
                            ∀ (u : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
                              c.mem[u]? = some m → m.loc = { label := l, offset := o + 1 } → m.val = w.val → t ≤ u
TermPtrRts.CntOK : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun T o w =>
  ∃ b S ws o', TermMem.SegOf T b S ∧ TermSound.ShapeC S ws ∧ ws[o']? = some w ∧ b + o' = o ∧ o' ∈ TermPtrRts.cntIdx S ws
TermPtrRts.CntPairOK : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → TermRun.Word → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun T o w0 w1 =>
  ∃ b S ws o',
    TermMem.SegOf T b S ∧
      TermSound.ShapeC S ws ∧ ws[o']? = some w0 ∧ ws[o' + 1]? = some w1 ∧ b + o' = o ∧ o' + 1 ∈ TermPtrRts.cntIdx S ws
TermPtrRts.EnvLbl : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Env → Prop
  := fun Eg env =>
  ∀ (x : String) (rt : CoreLang.Ty) (ws : List TermRun.Word),
    (x, rt, ws) ∈ env →
      ∀ (w : TermRun.Word),
        w ∈ TermPtrRts.pubWords rt ws →
          ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), TermPtrRts.Names l w → Eg.pfr l ≠ none ∨ Eg.box l ≠ none
TermPtrRts.EnvRts : WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → TermRun.Env → Prop
  := fun t a env =>
  ∀ (x : String) (rt : CoreLang.Ty) (ws : List TermRun.Word),
    (x, rt, ws) ∈ env → ∀ (w : TermRun.Word), w ∈ TermPtrRts.pubWords rt ws → TermPtrRts.Names a w → t ≤ w.rts
TermPtrRts.GateTmin : TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg Tot HP c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (a : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts),
    Eg.sto a = some cl →
      cl.owner = some j →
        Eg.pfr a = some f → 0 < (Tot j + HP j) (TermSound.Key.own a) → TermPtrRts.tminG Eg c j a ≠ none
TermPtrRts.GranuleW : TermPtrRts.Sto → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun sto mem pairs =>
  (∀ (q : WeakMem.Ts), q ∈ pairs → ∃ m0 m1, mem[q]? = some m0 ∧ mem[q + 1]? = some m1 ∧ m1.loc = m0.loc.next) ∧
    (∀ (q : WeakMem.Ts),
        q ∈ pairs →
          ∀ (v : Nat) (m0 mv : WeakMem.Msg),
            mem[q]? = some m0 → mem[v]? = some mv → mv.loc = m0.loc → q < v → v ∈ pairs) ∧
      (∀ (q : WeakMem.Ts),
          q ∈ pairs →
            ∀ (m0 : WeakMem.Msg) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
              mem[q]? = some m0 → sto m0.loc.label = some cl → TermStep.TwoAtT cl.ty m0.loc.offset) ∧
        ∀ (v : Nat) (mv : WeakMem.Msg) (cl : TermSound.Cell) (o : Nat),
          mem[v]? = some mv →
            sto mv.loc.label = some cl →
              mv.loc.offset = o + 1 →
                TermStep.TwoAtT cl.ty o →
                  (∀ (a : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat), mv.val ≠ WeakMem.Val.loc a g) ∨ ∃ q, q ∈ pairs ∧ v = q + 1
TermPtrRts.LblSto : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
    c.ths[i]? = some th → TermPtrRts.EnvLbl Eg th.env ∧ ∀ (f : TermRun.Frame), f ∈ th.stack → TermPtrRts.EnvLbl Eg f.env
TermPtrRts.MemRts : TermPtrRts.Sto → WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun sto t a mem P =>
  ∀ (u : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg), mem[u]? = some m → TermPtrRts.PlantEdge sto m a → ¬u ∈ P → t ≤ u
TermPtrRts.MemRtsC : TermPtrRts.Sto → WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun sto t a mem pairs P =>
  ∀ (u : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
    mem[u]? = some m →
      ¬u ∈ P →
        sto m.loc.label = some cl →
          TermPtrRts.Names a (TermMem.mw m) →
            (TermPtrRts.NotSnd cl.ty m.loc.offset ∧ TermPtrRts.CntOK cl.ty m.loc.offset (TermMem.mw m) ∨
                ∃ v m0,
                  v + 1 = u ∧
                    v ∈ pairs ∧
                      mem[v]? = some m0 ∧
                        m0.loc.label = m.loc.label ∧
                          TermPtrRts.CntPairOK cl.ty m0.loc.offset (TermMem.mw m0) (TermMem.mw m)) →
              t ≤ u
TermPtrRts.MemRtsCG : TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg Tot HP c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (a : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      Eg.sto a = some cl →
        cl.owner = some j →
          Eg.pfr a = some f →
            0 < (Tot j + HP j) (TermSound.Key.own a) →
              ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts),
                TermPtrRts.tminG Eg c j a = some t → TermPtrRts.MemRtsC Eg.sto t a c.mem c.pairs th.promises
TermPtrRts.Names : WeakMem.AllocLabel → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun a w => ∃ a' g, w.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a' g ∧ a'.label = a
TermPtrRts.NotSnd : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → Prop
  := fun T o =>
  ∀ (b : Nat) (S : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc) (oo : Nat) (u : TermRun.Acc) (d : Nat),
    TermMem.SegOf T b S → TermRun.accs S = some us → b + oo = o → TermPtrRts.UnitAt us oo = some (u, d) → d = 0
TermPtrRts.PlantEdge : TermPtrRts.Sto → WeakMem.Msg → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun sto m a =>
  (∃ ca, sto m.loc.label = some ca ∧ CoreLang.Ty.noOwnIn [] ca.ty = true ∧ TermSound.rgnFree ca.ty = true) ∧
    ∃ a' g, m.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a' g ∧ a'.label = a
TermPtrRts.PtrRtsG : TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg Tot HP c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (a : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      Eg.sto a = some cl →
        cl.owner = some j →
          Eg.pfr a = some f →
            0 < (Tot j + HP j) (TermSound.Key.own a) →
              ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts),
                TermPtrRts.tminG Eg c j a = some t →
                  TermPtrRts.MemRts Eg.sto t a c.mem th.promises ∧ TermPtrRts.ThRts t a th
TermPtrRts.PtrRtsGL : TermCut.GEnv → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg Tot HP c =>
  TermPtrRts.PtrRtsG Eg Tot HP c ∧
    TermPtrRts.LblSto Eg c ∧ TermPtrRts.GateTmin Eg Tot HP c ∧ TermPtrRts.MemRtsCG Eg Tot HP c
TermPtrRts.StackRts : WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → List TermRun.Frame → Prop
  := fun t a st => ∀ (f : TermRun.Frame), f ∈ st → TermPtrRts.EnvRts t a f.env
TermPtrRts.Sto : Type
  := WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option TermSound.Cell
TermPtrRts.ThRts : WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → TermRun.TTh → Prop
  := fun t a th => TermPtrRts.EnvRts t a th.env ∧ TermPtrRts.StackRts t a th.stack
TermPtrRts.UnitAt : List TermRun.Acc → Nat → Option (TermRun.Acc × Nat)
  eq_def ∀ (x : List TermRun.Acc) (x_1 : Nat),
  TermPtrRts.UnitAt x x_1 =
    match x, x_1 with
    | [], x => none
    | u :: us, i => if i < u.size then some (u, i) else TermPtrRts.UnitAt us (i - u.size)
TermPtrRts.cntIdx : CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → List Nat
  := fun S ws =>
  List.filterMap (fun w => if w.rts = 0 then none else some (w.rts - 1))
    (TermPtrRts.pubWords S (List.mapIdx (fun i w => { val := w.val, view := w.view, rts := i + 1 }) ws))
TermPtrRts.edgeB : TermPtrRts.Sto → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.Ts → Bool
  := fun sto s a t => decide (∃ m, s.mem[t]? = some m ∧ TermPtrRts.PlantEdge sto m a)
TermPtrRts.mPub : CoreLang.Mode → Bool
  := fun x =>
  match x with
  | CoreLang.Mode.pub => true
  | x => false
TermPtrRts.pubArm : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → List TermRun.Word → List TermRun.Word
  eq_def ∀ (x : CoreLang.Ty) (x_1 : Nat) (x_2 : List TermRun.Word),
  TermPtrRts.pubArm x x_1 x_2 =
    match x, x_1, x_2 with
    | a.sum b, j, p =>
      if j < (TermRun.armsOf a).length then TermPtrRts.pubArm a j p
      else TermPtrRts.pubArm b (j - (TermRun.armsOf a).length) p
    | t.refine ψ, j, p => TermPtrRts.pubArm t j p
    | t.iall, j, p => TermPtrRts.pubArm t j p
    | t.iex, j, p => TermPtrRts.pubArm t j p
    | t.mu, j, p => TermPtrRts.pubArm t j p
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t, j, p => TermPtrRts.pubArm t j p
    | t, x, p => TermPtrRts.pubWords t p
TermPtrRts.pubWords : CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → List TermRun.Word
  eq_def ∀ (x : CoreLang.Ty) (x_1 : List TermRun.Word),
  TermPtrRts.pubWords x x_1 =
    match x, x_1 with
    | a.prod b, ws =>
      if TermPtrRts.sNo (a.prod b) = true then ws
      else
        match TermSound.wc a with
        | some n => TermPtrRts.pubWords a (List.take n ws) ++ TermPtrRts.pubWords b (List.drop n ws)
        | none => []
    | a.sum b, ws =>
      if TermPtrRts.sNo (a.sum b) = true then ws
      else
        if (a.sum b).tier = CoreLang.Tier.boxed then ws
        else
          match TermRun.decodeArm (a.sum b).tier (TermRun.armsOf (TermSound.lnorm (a.sum b))) ws with
          | some (j, p) =>
            if j < (TermRun.armsOf a).length then TermPtrRts.pubArm a j p
            else TermPtrRts.pubArm b (j - (TermRun.armsOf a).length) p
          | none => []
    | t.refine ψ, ws => if TermPtrRts.sNo (t.refine ψ) = true then ws else TermPtrRts.pubWords t ws
    | t.iall, ws => if TermPtrRts.sNo t.iall = true then ws else TermPtrRts.pubWords t ws
    | t.iex, ws => if TermPtrRts.sNo t.iex = true then ws else TermPtrRts.pubWords t ws
    | t.mu, ws => if TermPtrRts.sNo t.mu = true then ws else TermPtrRts.pubWords t ws
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t, ws => if TermPtrRts.sNo (CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t) = true then ws else TermPtrRts.pubWords t ws
    | t, ws => if TermPtrRts.sNo t = true then ws else []
TermPtrRts.sNo : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  TermPtrRts.sNo x =
    match x with
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptr μ τ => TermPtrRts.mPub μ
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt μ τ => TermPtrRts.mPub μ
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc μ τ => TermPtrRts.mPub μ
    | a.prod b => TermPtrRts.sNo a && TermPtrRts.sNo b
    | a.sum b => TermPtrRts.sNo a && TermPtrRts.sNo b
    | t.refine ψ => TermPtrRts.sNo t
    | t.iall => TermPtrRts.sNo t
    | t.iex => TermPtrRts.sNo t
    | t.mu => TermPtrRts.sNo t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => κ.noOwn && TermPtrRts.sNo t
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => TermPtrRts.sNo t
    | x => true
TermPtrRts.tminG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Nat → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts
  := fun Eg c j a =>
  match c.ths[j]? with
  | some th => TermPtrRts.tminOf Eg.sto (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th) a
  | none => none
TermPtrRts.tminOf : TermPtrRts.Sto → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Option WeakMem.Ts
  := fun sto s a => (List.filter (TermPtrRts.edgeB sto s a) s.promises).min?
TermPtrStab.PromInCut : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (j : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    c.ths[j]? = some th →
      t ∈ th.promises →
        c.mem[t]? = some m →
          ∀ (cm : TermSound.Cell), Eg.sto m.loc.label = some cm → cm.owner = some j ∨ Eg.Shared m.loc.label

TermScope.AllDrf : List CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun σs => ∀ (σ : CoreLang.Ty), σ ∈ σs → TermScope.drf σ = true
TermScope.EnvDRF : TermRun.Env → CoreTerm.Expr → Prop
  := fun env t =>
  ∀ (x : String),
    TermScope.freeB x t = true →
      ∀ (rt : CoreLang.Ty) (ws : List TermRun.Word), TermRun.lookupE env x = some (rt, ws) → TermScope.drf rt = true
TermScope.FrDRF : TermRun.Frame → Prop
  | TermScope.FrDRF.mk : ∀ {f : TermRun.Frame},
  TermScope.allOK f.body = true →
    TermScope.AllDrf f.σs →
      TermScope.drf f.ty = true →
        (∀ (x : String),
            TermScope.freeB x f.body = true →
              x ≠ f.x →
                ∀ (rt : CoreLang.Ty) (ws : List TermRun.Word),
                  TermRun.lookupE f.env x = some (rt, ws) → TermScope.drf rt = true) →
          TermScope.FrDRF f
TermScope.ProgScopeOK : CoreTerm.Prog → Bool
  := fun p => List.all p fun d => TermScope.drf d.argTy && TermScope.drf d.retTy && TermScope.allOK d.body
TermScope.ProgScopeOKW : CoreTerm.Prog → Bool
  := fun p => TermScope.ProgScopeOK p && TermScope.ProgWrapOK p
TermScope.ProgWrapOK : CoreTerm.Prog → Bool
  := fun p => List.all p (TermScope.declWrapOK p)
TermScope.RunScope : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c => ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[i]? = some th → TermScope.ThScope th
TermScope.ThScope : TermRun.TTh → Prop
  | TermScope.ThScope.mk : ∀ {th : TermRun.TTh},
  TermScope.allOK th.term = true →
    TermScope.AllDrf th.σs →
      TermScope.EnvDRF th.env th.term → (∀ (f : TermRun.Frame), f ∈ th.stack → TermScope.FrDRF f) → TermScope.ThScope th
TermScope.allOK : CoreTerm.Expr → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (x : CoreTerm.Expr),
  TermScope.allOK x =
    match x with
    | CoreTerm.Expr.let_ x op e => TermScope.opOK op && TermScope.allOK e
    | CoreTerm.Expr.split a x y e => TermScope.allOK e
    | CoreTerm.Expr.unpack a i x e => TermScope.allOK e
    | CoreTerm.Expr.unpackT a α x e => TermScope.allOK e
    | CoreTerm.Expr.region a r p t e => !TermScope.freeB p e && TermScope.allOK e
    | CoreTerm.Expr.openPub a r p c e => !TermScope.freeB p e && TermScope.allOK e
    | CoreTerm.Expr.case a x e₁ y e₂ => TermScope.allOK e₁ && TermScope.allOK e₂
    | CoreTerm.Expr.ifnull a e₁ x e₂ => TermScope.allOK e₁ && TermScope.allOK e₂
    | CoreTerm.Expr.parity a k e₁ e₂ => TermScope.allOK e₁ && TermScope.allOK e₂
    | CoreTerm.Expr.ifeq a b e₁ e₂ => TermScope.allOK e₁ && TermScope.allOK e₂
    | CoreTerm.Expr.cas acq rel w e v t ok fail => TermScope.allOK ok && TermScope.allOK fail
    | CoreTerm.Expr.casp acq rel w e0 e1 v0 v1 t ok fail => TermScope.allOK ok && TermScope.allOK fail
    | CoreTerm.Expr.ret a => true
    | CoreTerm.Expr.tail f is a ts => ts.all TermScope.drf
TermScope.argOK : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  := fun t => TermScope.wrapOK t && !TermScope.rcar t
TermScope.checkW : CoreTerm.Prog → CoreTerm.Cx → CoreTerm.Expr → CoreTerm.M Unit
  eq_def ∀ (p : CoreTerm.Prog) (cx : CoreTerm.Cx) (x : CoreTerm.Expr),
  TermScope.checkW p cx x =
    match x with
    | CoreTerm.Expr.let_ x op e =>
      have __do_jp := fun __r => do
        let cx ← CoreTerm.checkOp p cx x op
        have __do_jp : Unit → CoreTerm.M Unit := fun __r => TermScope.checkW p cx e
        match List.find? (fun x_1 => x_1.fst == x) cx.vars with
          | some (fst, t, snd) =>
            if (!TermScope.wrapOK t) = true then do
              let __r ← throw (toString "A1: " ++ toString x ++ toString " has a key-carrying wrapper type")
              __do_jp __r
            else __do_jp ()
          | none => __do_jp ();
      if (!TermScope.opArgOK op) = true then do
        let __r ← throw "A1: a type argument or witness carries a key"
        __do_jp __r
      else __do_jp ()
    | CoreTerm.Expr.split a x y e => do
      let __x ← cx.use a
      match __x with
        | (t, cx) =>
          match t with
          | u.prod v => do
            let cx ← cx.bind x u
            let cx ← cx.bind y v
            TermScope.checkW p cx e
          | x => throw "split needs a pair"
    | CoreTerm.Expr.unpack a i x e => do
      let __x ← cx.use a
      match __x with
        | (t, cx) =>
          match t with
          | b.iex => do
            let cx ← (cx.push i).bind x b
            TermScope.checkW p cx e
          | x => throw "unpack needs an index existential"
    | CoreTerm.Expr.unpackT a α x e => do
      let __x ← cx.use a
      match __x with
        | (t, cx) =>
          match t with
          | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ b =>
            have __do_jp := fun __r => do
              let cx ← (cx.pushT κ).bind x b
              TermScope.checkW p cx e;
            if (!CoreLang.Ty.guardT 0 b) = true then do
              let __r ← throw "unpack: the package's variable must occur only under a reference"
              __do_jp __r
            else __do_jp ()
          | x => throw "unpack at a type needs a type existential"
    | CoreTerm.Expr.region a rn pn tn e => do
      let __x ← cx.use a
      match __x with
        | (t, cx) =>
          match t with
          | CoreLang.Ty.ptr CoreLang.Mode.own u =>
            have __do_jp := fun __r =>
              have cx := cx.push rn;
              do
              let cx ← cx.bind pn (CoreLang.Ty.ptr (CoreLang.Mode.rgn (CoreLang.Idx.var 0)) (CoreLang.Ty.shiftI 0 u))
              let cx ← cx.bind tn (CoreLang.Ty.tok (CoreLang.Idx.var 0) none)
              TermScope.checkW p cx e;
            if (!CoreTerm.rgnCellOK cx.ke u) = true then do
              let __r ←
                throw
                    "region: the cell type is not shareable (rows 16, 17): an own reference inside a region must be moved in with intoRgn"
              __do_jp __r
            else __do_jp ()
          | (CoreLang.Ty.loc CoreLang.Mode.own (CoreLang.Ty.array n u)).refine (CoreLang.Pred.ini k) =>
            have __do_jp := fun __r =>
              have cx := cx.push rn;
              do
              let cx ←
                cx.bind pn
                    ((CoreLang.Ty.loc (CoreLang.Mode.rgn (CoreLang.Idx.var 0))
                          (CoreLang.Ty.shiftI 0 (CoreLang.Ty.array n u))).refine
                      (CoreLang.Pred.shift 0 (CoreLang.Pred.ini k)))
              let cx ← cx.bind tn (CoreLang.Ty.tok (CoreLang.Idx.var 0) none)
              TermScope.checkW p cx e;
            if (!CoreTerm.rgnCellOK cx.ke (CoreLang.Ty.array n u)) = true then do
              let __r ←
                throw
                    "region: the cell type is not shareable (rows 16, 17): an own reference inside a region must be moved in with intoRgn"
              __do_jp __r
            else __do_jp ()
          | x => throw "region needs an own reference or an own buffer"
    | CoreTerm.Expr.openPub a rn pn cn e => do
      let __x ← cx.use a
      match __x with
        | (t, cx) =>
          match t with
          | CoreLang.Ty.ptr CoreLang.Mode.pub u =>
            have cx := cx.push rn;
            do
            let cx ← cx.bind pn (CoreLang.Ty.ptr (CoreLang.Mode.rgn (CoreLang.Idx.var 0)) (CoreLang.Ty.shiftI 0 u))
            let cx ← cx.bind cn (CoreLang.Ty.cap CoreLang.Cap.pub (CoreLang.Idx.var 0))
            TermScope.checkW p cx e
          | x => throw "openPub needs a pub reference"
    | CoreTerm.Expr.case a x e₁ y e₂ => do
      let __x ← cx.use a
      match __x with
        | (t, cx) =>
          match t with
          | u.sum v => do
            let __do_lift ← cx.bind x u
            TermScope.checkW p __do_lift e₁
            let __do_lift ← cx.bind y v
            TermScope.checkW p __do_lift e₂
          | x => throw "case needs a sum"
    | CoreTerm.Expr.ifnull a e₁ y e₂ => do
      let __x ← cx.use a
      match __x with
        | (t, cx) =>
          match t with
          | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt μ u => do
            TermScope.checkW p cx e₁
            let __do_lift ← cx.bind y (CoreLang.Ty.ptr μ u)
            TermScope.checkW p __do_lift e₂
          | x => throw "ifnull needs a nullable reference"
    | CoreTerm.Expr.parity a k ev od => do
      let i ← CoreTerm.intIdx cx a
      have __do_jp : Nat → CoreTerm.M Unit := fun v =>
        have cx := cx.push k;
        do
        TermScope.checkW p
            { inames := cx.inames, facts := (v + 1, CoreLang.Idx.smul 2 (CoreLang.Idx.var 0)) :: cx.facts,
              vars := cx.vars, saws := cx.saws, torn := cx.torn, unsealed := cx.unsealed, ke := cx.ke, opq := cx.opq,
              lent := cx.lent, ret := cx.ret }
            ev
        TermScope.checkW p
            { inames := cx.inames,
              facts := (v + 1, (CoreLang.Idx.smul 2 (CoreLang.Idx.var 0)).add (CoreLang.Idx.lit 1)) :: cx.facts,
              vars := cx.vars, saws := cx.saws, torn := cx.torn, unsealed := cx.unsealed, ke := cx.ke, opq := cx.opq,
              lent := cx.lent, ret := cx.ret }
            od
      match (cx.norm i).bind CoreTerm.Lin.asVar? with
        | some v => do
          let v ← pure v
          __do_jp v
        | none => do
          let v ← throw "parity: the index is not a free variable, so there is nothing to refine"
          __do_jp v
    | CoreTerm.Expr.ifeq a b yes no =>
      if CoreTerm.plainEq cx a b = true then do
        TermScope.checkW p cx yes
        TermScope.checkW p cx no
      else do
        let i ← CoreTerm.intIdx cx a
        let j ← CoreTerm.intIdx cx b
        match cx.norm i, cx.norm j with
