  { term := CoreTerm.Expr.tail f [] (CoreTerm.At.var "a") ts,
    env := [("a", T, List.map (TermSpawn.bumpW (TermSpawnStep.spawnS c th).vs.vRelSt) ws)],
    vs :=
      have __src := (TermSpawnStep.spawnS c th).vs;
      { coh := __src.coh, vRd := __src.vRd, vWr := __src.vWr, vCtrl := __src.vCtrl, vPreBase := __src.vPreBase,
        vRelSt := __src.vRelSt, fwd := __src.fwd },
    tidLbl := some (TermSpawnStep.tidLabel c i th) }
TermSpawnStep.spawnPar : TermRun.Cfg → Nat → TermRun.TTh → String → CoreTerm.Expr → TermRun.TTh
  := fun c i th x e =>
  have __src := TermRun.withS th (TermSpawnStep.spawnS c th);
  { term := e,
    env :=
      (x, CoreLang.Ty.tid, [TermRun.cst (WeakMem.Val.loc { label := TermSpawnStep.tidLabel c i th, offset := 0 } 0)]) ::
        th.env,
    σs := __src.σs, stack := __src.stack, vs := __src.vs, promises := __src.promises,
    nalloc := th.nalloc + if th.tidLbl = none then 2 else 1, undefs := __src.undefs,
    tidLbl := th.tidLbl.orElse fun x => some (TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase c.n i (th.nalloc + 1)),
    permit := __src.permit }
TermSpawnStep.spawnS : TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.TTh → TermRun.St
  := fun c th => TermRun.fenceS (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th) WeakMem.Fence.sy
TermSpawnStep.spawnT : TermRun.Cfg → Nat → TermRun.TTh → TermRun.TTh → TermRun.Cfg
  := fun c i par kid =>
  { mem := c.mem, pairs := c.pairs, ths := (c.ths.set! i par).push kid, lbase := c.lbase, boxFence := c.boxFence,
    cap := c.cap, trace := c.trace }
TermSpawnStep.tidLabel : TermRun.Cfg → Nat → TermRun.TTh → WeakMem.AllocLabel
  := fun c i th => TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase c.n i th.nalloc
TermStep.AboveFW : WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun mem a t => ∃ h, TermStep.FirstW mem a h ∧ h < t
TermStep.AtomT : CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun U => (∀ (a b : CoreLang.Ty), U ≠ a.prod b) ∧ ∀ (t : CoreLang.Ty), U ≠ t.mu
TermStep.BlkDb : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Address → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E mem a Z => ∃ Zb Zz, TermStep.BlkZ E mem a Zb ∧ TermStep.ZwZ E a Zz ∧ Z = TermStep.noOwnR (Zb + Zz)
TermStep.BlkStart : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun E a U =>
  ∃ c N e us j o w,
    E.sto a.label = some c ∧
      c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
        TermRun.accs e = some us ∧
          (∀ (u : TermRun.Acc), u ∈ us → u = TermRun.Acc.one) ∧
            j < N ∧
              a.offset = j * TermRun.nWords us + o ∧
                TermSound.AtOff e o U ∧
                  TermStep.AtomT U ∧
                    TermSound.wc U = some w ∧
                      0 < w ∧ ∀ (r : CoreLang.Idx) (P : CoreLang.Proto), U = CoreLang.Ty.word r P → P.ghost = true
TermStep.BlkZ : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Address → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E mem a Z =>
  (∃ U w ws,
      TermStep.BlkStart E a U ∧
        TermSound.wc U = some w ∧
          ws.length = w ∧
            (∀ (o : WeakMem.Offset) (x : TermRun.Word),
                ws[o]? = some x →
                  ∃ h m,
                    TermStep.FirstW mem { label := a.label, offset := a.offset + o } h ∧
                      mem[h]? = some m ∧ x = TermMem.mw m) ∧
              ∃ E0,
                (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box), E0.box l = some B → E.box l = some B) ∧
                  TermSound.VRR (fun x => 0) (TermSound.CE E0.unsee) U (TermSound.shp U) ws Z) ∨
    (∀ (U : CoreLang.Ty), ¬TermStep.BlkStart E a U) ∧ Z = TermSound.Res.zero
TermStep.BoxInv : TermRun.G → TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun g E s =>
  (∀ (j : Nat), s.nalloc ≤ j → E.box (TermRun.freshLabel g.lbase g.n g.i j) = none) ∧
    TermStep.BoxTopP E s.mem s.promises ∧
      ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
        E.box l = some B →
          E.sto l = none ∧
            E.ini l = 0 ∧
              (E.seen l →
                TermStep.BoxWords B fun o w => TermStep.LatestP s.mem s.promises s.vs.coh { label := l, offset := o } w)
TermStep.BoxTopP : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun E mem P =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
    E.box l = some B →
      TermStep.BoxWords B fun o w => ∃ t, t ≤ B.fr ∧ TermStep.LatestP mem P (fun x => t) { label := l, offset := o } w
TermStep.BoxWords : TermSound.Box → (Nat → TermRun.Word → Prop) → Prop
  := fun B Q => Q 0 (TermRun.cst (WeakMem.Val.num B.tag)) ∧ ∀ (o : Nat) (w : TermRun.Word), B.pay[o]? = some w → Q (o + 1) w
TermStep.BufOKR : TermSound.TEnv → Nat → Nat → CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun η n nr T vals R =>
  ∃ N e us Rs,
    T = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
      TermRun.accs e = some us ∧
        n ≤ N ∧
          vals.length = N * TermRun.nWords us ∧
            Rs.length = n ∧
              R = TermSound.Res.sum Rs ∧
                ∀ (j : Nat) (Rj : TermSound.Res),
                  j < n →
                    Rs[j]? = some Rj →
                      (nr ≤ j → TermRun.nWords us = 0 → Rj = TermSound.Res.zero) ∧
                        (j < nr ∨ 0 < TermRun.nWords us →
                          TermStep.ValOfR η e (List.take (TermRun.nWords us) (List.drop (j * TermRun.nWords us) vals))
                            Rj)
TermStep.CellOKR : TermSound.TEnv → Nat → Nat → CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun η n nr T vals R => TermSound.wc T = some vals.length ∧ TermStep.ValOfR η T vals R ∨ TermStep.BufOKR η n nr T vals R
TermStep.ClR : (WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop) → WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  | TermStep.ClR.base : ∀ {R : WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop} {x : WeakMem.AllocLabel}, TermStep.ClR R x x
  | TermStep.ClR.step : ∀ {R : WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop} {x l a : WeakMem.AllocLabel},
  TermStep.ClR R x l → R l a → TermStep.ClR R x a
TermStep.DStart : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a =>
  (∃ c, E.sto a.label = some c ∧ c.owner = some E.tid) ∧ ((∃ U, TermStep.BlkStart E a U) ∨ TermStep.SlotStart E a)
TermStep.DbRes : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Msg → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E mem m tw R =>
  TermStep.Vis E m.loc.label ∧
      TermStep.IniW E m.loc ∧
        (TermStep.InitAt mem m.loc tw →
            ∃ R₁ Z,
              TermStep.FRes E m.loc (TermMem.mw m) R₁ ∧
                (TermStep.DesigAt E mem m.loc tw → TermStep.BlkDb E mem m.loc Z) ∧
                  (¬TermStep.DesigAt E mem m.loc tw → Z = TermSound.Res.zero) ∧ R = R₁ + Z) ∧
          (¬TermStep.InitAt mem m.loc tw → TermStep.WRes E m.loc (TermMem.mw m) R) ∨
    ¬(TermStep.Vis E m.loc.label ∧ TermStep.IniW E m.loc) ∧ R = TermSound.Res.zero
TermStep.DesigAt : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun E mem a tw => TermStep.PredU E a ∧ TermStep.DStart E a ∧ TermStep.FirstW mem a tw
TermStep.EmptyZ : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun E l =>
  ∃ c N e us,
    E.sto l = some c ∧
      c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
        TermRun.accs e = some us ∧ TermRun.nWords us = 0 ∧ ¬TermStep.InhZ E.prog e
TermStep.FRes : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → TermRun.Word → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E a w R =>
  (∃ n P, TermStep.WordAt E a n P ∧ R = TermStep.wr P n w.val + TermSound.wdW P n) ∨
    (∀ (n : Nat) (P : CoreLang.Proto), ¬TermStep.WordAt E a n P) ∧ R = TermSound.Res.zero
TermStep.FirstW : WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun mem a h =>
  (∃ m, mem[h]? = some m ∧ m.loc = a ∧ m.val ≠ WeakMem.Val.undef) ∧
    ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg), t < h → mem[t]? = some m → m.loc = a → m.val = WeakMem.Val.undef
TermStep.FpAt : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → List WeakMem.Ts
  := fun E a => if TermStep.Vis E a.label then E.fp else []
TermStep.FpOK : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun E s =>
  (∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts), t ∈ E.fp → t < Array.size s.mem) ∧
    ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg), t ∈ E.fp → s.mem[t]? = some m → TermStep.Vis E m.loc.label
TermStep.FreshLP : TermRun.G → TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun g E s =>
  (∀ (j : Nat),
      s.nalloc ≤ j →
        E.sto (TermRun.freshLabel g.lbase g.n g.i j) = none ∧
          (∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
              s.mem[t]? = some m → m.loc.label = TermRun.freshLabel g.lbase g.n g.i j → t ∈ s.promises) ∧
            E.ini (TermRun.freshLabel g.lbase g.n g.i j) = 0 ∧ E.pfr (TermRun.freshLabel g.lbase g.n g.i j) = none) ∧
    TermStep.PrivCoh E s
TermStep.HeldEntry : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E s l R =>
  (E.sto l = none → R = TermSound.Res.zero) ∧
    ∀ (c : TermSound.Cell),
      E.sto l = some c →
        ∃ vals,
          (∀ (o : WeakMem.Offset) (w : TermRun.Word),
              vals[o]? = some w →
                TermStep.LatestNP s.mem (TermStep.PromAt E s { label := l, offset := o }) { label := l, offset := o }
                  w) ∧
            TermStep.CellOKR E (E.ini l) (E.rc l) c.ty vals R
TermStep.HiddenP : TermSound.TEnv → CoreLang.Ty → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun η T mem P l o V =>
  ∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    ¬t ∈ P →
      mem[t]? = some m →
        m.loc = { label := l, offset := o } →
          ¬TermMem.WordOK η T o (TermMem.mw m) →
            ∃ t' m', t < t' ∧ t' ≤ V ∧ mem[t']? = some m' ∧ m'.loc = { label := l, offset := o }
TermStep.HoldW : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → Prop
  := fun E s l f =>
  ∃ t,
    t ∈ s.promises ∧
      ∃ m,
        s.mem[t]? = some m ∧
          m.loc.label = l ∧
            f ≤ t ∧
              (∀ (u : WeakMem.Ts),
                  u ∈ s.promises → ∀ (m' : WeakMem.Msg), s.mem[u]? = some m' → m'.loc = m.loc → t ≤ u) ∧
                ¬TermStep.IniWr E m.loc
TermStep.InAcc : List TermRun.Acc → Nat → Nat → Prop
  := fun us i o => TermRun.nWords (List.take i us) ≤ o ∧ o < TermRun.nWords (List.take (i + 1) us)
TermStep.InWords : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → Prop
  := fun T o =>
  (∀ (L : List TermRun.Acc), TermRun.accs T = some L → o < TermRun.nWords L) ∧
    ∀ (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
      T = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e → TermRun.accs e = some us → o < N * TermRun.nWords us
TermStep.Inh : TermSound.TEnv → Prop
  := fun E =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (b : Nat) (S : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    E.sto l = some c →
      TermMem.SegOf c.ty b S →
        TermRun.accs S = some us → TermStep.IniSeg E c l b → ∃ ws, TermMem.ValOf E S ws ∧ ws.length = TermRun.nWords us
TermStep.InhZ : CoreTerm.Prog → CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun P e => ∃ ws, TermMem.ValOf { prog := P } e ws ∧ ws.length = 0
TermStep.IniSeg : TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.Cell → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → Prop
  := fun E c l b => TermStep.IniSegN (E.ini l) c b
TermStep.IniSegN : Nat → TermSound.Cell → Nat → Prop
  := fun n c b =>
  ∀ (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty),
    c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
      ∃ us j, TermRun.accs e = some us ∧ j < n ∧ b = j * TermRun.nWords us
TermStep.IniW : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a =>
  ∀ (c : TermSound.Cell) (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    E.sto a.label = some c →
      c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
        TermRun.accs e = some us → a.offset < E.ini a.label * TermRun.nWords us
TermStep.IniWr : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a =>
  ∀ (c : TermSound.Cell) (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    E.sto a.label = some c →
      c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
        TermRun.accs e = some us → a.offset < E.rc a.label * TermRun.nWords us
TermStep.InitAt : WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun mem a tw => ∀ (w : TermRun.Word), TermStep.PredAt mem a tw w → w.val = WeakMem.Val.undef
TermStep.InitRr : TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.Cell → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → Prop
  := fun η c l o =>
  ∀ (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e → TermRun.accs e = some us → o < η.rc l * TermRun.nWords us
TermStep.LatestNP : WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.Address → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun mem P a w =>
  ∃ t m,
    ¬t ∈ P ∧
      mem[t]? = some m ∧
        m.loc = a ∧
          TermMem.mw m = w ∧ ∀ (t' : WeakMem.Ts) (m' : WeakMem.Msg), t < t' → mem[t']? = some m' → m'.loc = a → t' ∈ P
TermStep.LatestP : WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → (WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts) → WeakMem.Address → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun mem P coh a w => TermStep.LatestS mem (fun x => x ∈ P) coh a w
TermStep.LatestS : WeakMem.Memory → (WeakMem.Ts → Prop) → (WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts) → WeakMem.Address → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun mem S coh a w =>
  (∃ m,
      mem[coh a]? = some m ∧
        m.loc = a ∧ m.val = w.val ∧ ∀ (x : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat), w.val = WeakMem.Val.loc x g → w.view ≤ m.view) ∧
    ¬S (coh a) ∧ ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg), coh a < t → mem[t]? = some m → m.loc = a → S t
TermStep.LatestSF : TermSound.TEnv →
  TermSound.Cell →
    Option WeakMem.Ts →
      WeakMem.Memory → (WeakMem.Ts → Prop) → (WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts) → WeakMem.Address → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun η c fr mem S coh a w =>
  (∃ m,
      mem[coh a]? = some m ∧
        m.loc = a ∧
          ((m.val = w.val ∧ ∀ (x : WeakMem.Address) (g : Nat), w.val = WeakMem.Val.loc x g → w.view ≤ m.view) ∨
            fr ≠ none ∧ TermStep.TypedAt η c m)) ∧
    ¬S (coh a) ∧
      ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
        coh a < t → mem[t]? = some m → m.loc = a → S t ∨ fr ≠ none ∧ TermStep.TypedAt η c m
TermStep.LowestIn : WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun mem P a t =>
  t ∈ P ∧
    (∃ m, mem[t]? = some m ∧ m.loc = a) ∧ ∀ (t' : WeakMem.Ts), t' ∈ P → (∃ m, mem[t']? = some m ∧ m.loc = a) → t ≤ t'
TermStep.MemInv : TermRun.G → TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun g E s =>
  TermStep.MemOKP E s.mem s.pairs s.promises ∧
    TermStep.OwnOKPF E s.mem s.pairs s.vs s.promises ∧
      TermStep.PromLt s ∧
        TermStep.FreshLP g E s ∧
          TermMem.SeenLE E s.vs.vRelSt ∧
            TermStep.OwnHidP E s.mem s.vs s.promises ∧
              TermStep.PubDefP E s.mem s.promises ∧
                TermStep.OwnPubV E s ∧ TermStep.PubInv E s ∧ TermStep.BoxInv g E s ∧ TermStep.PromOK s
TermStep.MemOKP : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun η mem pairs P =>
  (∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m : WeakMem.Msg) (c : TermSound.Cell),
      ¬t ∈ P →
        mem[t]? = some m →
          η.sto m.loc.label = some c → m.val = WeakMem.Val.undef ∨ TermMem.WordOK η c.ty m.loc.offset (TermMem.mw m)) ∧
    (∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts) (m0 m1 : WeakMem.Msg) (c : TermSound.Cell),
        t ∈ pairs →
          mem[t]? = some m0 →
            mem[t + 1]? = some m1 →
              η.sto m0.loc.label = some c → TermMem.PairOK η c.ty m0.loc.offset (TermMem.mw m0) (TermMem.mw m1)) ∧
      ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts), t ∈ pairs → t + 1 < Array.size mem
TermStep.NoHold : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun E s l =>
  (∀ (f : WeakMem.Ts), E.pfr l = some f → ¬E.seen l → ¬TermStep.HoldW E s l f) ∧
    (TermStep.ZW E l ∨ E.ini l ≤ E.rini l) ∧ (E.pfr l = none → ∀ (u : Nat), ¬TermStep.HoldW E s l u)
TermStep.Np : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat
  := fun E s l =>
  match E.sto l with
  | some c =>
    List.foldr max 0
      (List.filter (fun n => decide (TermStep.PrefixHeld E s l c n)) (List.range (TermStep.slotCnt c + 1)))
  | none => 0
TermStep.NpZ : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat
  := fun E s l => if TermStep.EmptyZ E l then 0 else TermStep.Np E s l
TermStep.OldCr : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.Msg → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E s m tw R =>
  (∃ w, TermStep.PredAt s.mem m.loc tw w ∧ TermStep.VRes E m.loc w R) ∨
    (∀ (w : TermRun.Word), ¬TermStep.PredAt s.mem m.loc tw w) ∧ R = TermSound.Res.zero
TermStep.OwnHidP : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.VState → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun η mem vs P =>
  (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell),
      η.sto l = some c →
        c.owner = some η.tid → ∀ (o : Nat), TermStep.InitRr η c l o → TermStep.HiddenP η c.ty mem P l o vs.vWr) ∧
    ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell),
      η.sto l = some c →
        c.owner = some η.tid →
          η.rc l < η.ini l →
            ∃ f,
              η.pfr l = some f ∧
                (¬TermStep.ZW η l → ¬η.seen l) ∧
                  ∀ (o : Nat), TermMem.InitR η c l o → TermStep.HiddenP η c.ty mem P l o f
TermStep.OwnL : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun E l => ∃ c, E.sto l = some c ∧ c.owner = some E.tid
TermStep.OwnOKPF : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.VState → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun η mem pairs vs P => TermStep.OwnOKSF η mem pairs vs fun x => x ∈ P
TermStep.OwnOKSF : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.VState → (WeakMem.Ts → Prop) → Prop
  := fun η mem pairs vs S =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell),
    η.sto l = some c →
      c.owner = some η.tid →
        ∃ vals,
          TermMem.CellOK η (η.rc l) c.ty vals ∧
            (∀ (o : WeakMem.Offset) (v : TermRun.Word),
                vals[o]? = some v → TermStep.LatestSF η c (η.pfr l) mem S vs.coh { label := l, offset := o } v) ∧
              (η.pfr l ≠ none → TermStep.OwnPairF η c l vals mem pairs S vs.coh)
TermStep.OwnPairF : TermSound.TEnv →
  TermSound.Cell →
    WeakMem.AllocLabel →
      List TermRun.Word → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → (WeakMem.Ts → Prop) → (WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts) → Prop
  := fun η c l vals mem pairs S coh =>
  ∀ (o : Nat),
    TermStep.TwoAtT c.ty o →
      TermStep.InitRr η c l o →
        ∀ (t0 t1 : Nat) (m0 m1 : WeakMem.Msg),
          ¬S t0 →
            ¬S t1 →
              mem[t0]? = some m0 →
                mem[t1]? = some m1 →
                  m0.loc = { label := l, offset := o } →
                    m1.loc = { label := l, offset := o + 1 } →
                      coh { label := l, offset := o } ≤ t0 →
                        coh { label := l, offset := o + 1 } ≤ t1 →
                          WeakMem.pairStraddles mem pairs { label := l, offset := o } t0 t1 = false →
                            (t0 = coh { label := l, offset := o } ∧
                                t1 = coh { label := l, offset := o + 1 } ∧
                                  (∀ (v : TermRun.Word), vals[o]? = some v → TermSound.WLe1 v (TermMem.mw m0)) ∧
                                    ∀ (v : TermRun.Word), vals[o + 1]? = some v → TermSound.WLe1 v (TermMem.mw m1)) ∨
                              TermMem.PairOK η c.ty o (TermMem.mw m0) (TermMem.mw m1)
TermStep.OwnPubV : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun E s =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts),
    E.sto l = some c → c.owner = some E.tid → E.pfr l = some f → E.seen l ∨ f ≤ s.vs.vWr ∨ TermStep.HoldW E s l f
TermStep.PinW : WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Nat → List TermRun.Acc → (Nat → Bool) → List TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun mem W l b us K ws =>
  ∀ (i o : Nat) (w : TermRun.Word) (t' : WeakMem.Ts),
    K i = true →
      TermStep.InAcc us i o →
        ws[o]? = some w →
          TermStep.LowestIn mem W { label := l, offset := b + o } t' →
            TermStep.PredAt mem { label := l, offset := b + o } t' w
TermStep.PlantCr : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.Msg → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E s m tw R =>
  ∃ c sb S0 b0 S us wb,
    E.sto m.loc.label = some c ∧
      c.owner = some E.tid ∧
        E.pfr m.loc.label ≠ none ∧
          TermMem.SegOf c.ty sb S0 ∧
            TermSound.AtOff S0 b0 S ∧
              TermStep.Solid S ∧
                ¬TermStep.WordUnit E m.loc ∧
                  TermRun.accs S = some us ∧
                    0 < TermRun.nWords us ∧
                      sb + b0 ≤ m.loc.offset ∧
                        m.loc.offset < sb + b0 + TermRun.nWords us ∧
                          tw ≤ wb ∧
                            wb < Array.size s.mem ∧
                              (∀ (tf : WeakMem.Ts) (mf : WeakMem.Msg),
                                  tf ∈ TermStep.FpAt E { label := m.loc.label, offset := 0 } →
                                    s.mem[tf]? = some mf → mf.loc.label = m.loc.label → wb < tf) ∧
                                (∀ (t' : WeakMem.Ts) (m' : WeakMem.Msg),
                                    t' ∈ s.promises →
                                      s.mem[t']? = some m' →
                                        m'.loc.label = m.loc.label →
                                          sb + b0 ≤ m'.loc.offset →
                                            m'.loc.offset < sb + b0 + TermRun.nWords us → t' ≤ wb → tw ≤ t') ∧
                                  ((∃ K ws R0,
                                      ws.length = TermRun.nWords us ∧
                                        TermStep.ValOfR E S ws R0 ∧
                                          R0 ≤ R ∧
                                            TermStep.PinW s.mem (TermStep.winL wb s.promises) m.loc.label (sb + b0) us K
                                              ws) ∨
                                    R = TermSound.Res.zero)
TermStep.PredAt : WeakMem.Memory → WeakMem.Address → WeakMem.Ts → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun mem a t w =>
  ∃ t0 m,
    t0 < t ∧
      mem[t0]? = some m ∧
        m.loc = a ∧
          TermMem.mw m = w ∧ ∀ (t' : WeakMem.Ts) (m' : WeakMem.Msg), t0 < t' → t' < t → mem[t']? = some m' → m'.loc ≠ a
TermStep.PredU : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a => E.pfr a.label ≠ none ∧ TermStep.IniW E a ∧ ¬TermStep.IniWr E a
TermStep.PrefixHeld : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → TermSound.Cell → Nat → Prop
  := fun E s l c k =>
  ∀ (Na : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit Na) e →
      TermRun.accs e = some us →
        ∀ (o : Nat),
          o < k * TermRun.nWords us →
            TermMem.InitR E c l o ∨ ∃ u, u ∈ s.promises ∧ ∃ m, s.mem[u]? = some m ∧ m.loc = { label := l, offset := o }
TermStep.Priv : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun E l => TermStep.OwnL E l ∧ E.pfr l = none
TermStep.PrivCoh : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun E s =>
  ∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg),
    ¬t ∈ s.promises → s.mem[t]? = some m → TermStep.PrivU E m.loc → t = s.vs.coh m.loc ∧ m.val = WeakMem.Val.undef
TermStep.PrivU : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a =>
  ∃ c N e us,
    E.sto a.label = some c ∧
      c.owner = some E.tid ∧
        E.pfr a.label = none ∧
          c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
            TermRun.accs e = some us ∧ E.rc a.label * TermRun.nWords us ≤ a.offset ∧ a.offset < N * TermRun.nWords us
TermStep.PromAbove : TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun s => ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts), t ∈ s.promises → ∀ (m : WeakMem.Msg), s.mem[t]? = some m → s.vs.coh m.loc < t
TermStep.PromAt : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.Address → List WeakMem.Ts
  := fun E s a =>
  if TermStep.VisU E a then []
  else
    if TermStep.PredU E a then
      List.filter (fun t => decide (TermStep.AboveFW s.mem a t)) (s.promises ++ TermStep.FpAt E a)
    else s.promises ++ TermStep.FpAt E a
TermStep.PromLt : TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun s => ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts), t ∈ s.promises → t < Array.size s.mem
TermStep.PromOK : TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun s =>
  s.promises.Nodup ∧
    (∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts), t ∈ s.promises → ¬t ∈ s.pairs) ∧
      ∀ (t : WeakMem.Ts), t ∈ s.promises → ∀ (p : WeakMem.Ts), p ∈ s.pairs → t ≠ p + 1
TermStep.PubCpl : TermSound.TEnv → Prop
  := fun E =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts),
    E.sto l = some c →
      E.pfr l = some f →
        TermSound.LayT c.ty ∧
          (∀ (b : Nat) (S : CoreLang.Ty), TermMem.SegOf c.ty b S → TermRun.accs S ≠ none → TermStep.TearOK S) ∧
            CoreLang.Ty.coupledOKIn [] c.ty = true ∧ CoreLang.Ty.noOwnIn [] c.ty = true ∧ TermSound.rgnFree c.ty = true
TermStep.PubDefP : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun η mem P =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts),
    η.sto l = some c →
      c.owner ≠ some η.tid → η.pfr l = some f → ∀ (o : Nat), TermMem.InitR η c l o → TermStep.HiddenP η c.ty mem P l o f
TermStep.PubInv : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun E s => TermStep.SeenOwn E ∧ TermStep.Inh E ∧ TermStep.PubCpl E ∧ TermStep.PubMem E s.mem s.pairs s.promises
TermStep.PubLive : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → Prop
  := fun E mem =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts) (b : Nat) (S : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc)
    (o : Nat),
    E.sto l = some c →
      c.owner ≠ some E.tid →
        E.pfr l = some f →
          TermMem.SegOf c.ty b S →
            TermRun.accs S = some us →
              o < TermRun.nWords us → ∃ t m, mem[t]? = some m ∧ m.loc = { label := l, offset := b + o }
TermStep.PubMem : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun E mem pairs P => TermStep.PubPair E mem pairs P ∧ TermStep.PubLive E mem
TermStep.PubPair : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → List WeakMem.Ts → Prop
  := fun E mem pairs P =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts) (o : Nat),
    E.sto l = some c →
      ((c.owner ≠ some E.tid ∧
            ∃ b S us o',
              TermMem.SegOf c.ty b S ∧
                TermRun.accs S = some us ∧ TermStep.TwoAt us o' ∧ o = b + o' ∧ TermStep.IniSegN (E.ini l) c b) ∨
          E.rc l < E.ini l ∧
            ∃ b S us o',
              TermMem.SegOf c.ty b S ∧
                TermRun.accs S = some us ∧
                  TermStep.TwoAt us o' ∧ o = b + o' ∧ ¬TermStep.IniSegN (E.rc l) c b ∧ TermStep.IniSegN (E.ini l) c b) →
        E.pfr l = some f →
          TermStep.TwoAtT c.ty o →
            ∀ (t0 t1 : Nat) (m0 m1 : WeakMem.Msg),
              ¬t0 ∈ P →
                ¬t1 ∈ P →
                  mem[t0]? = some m0 →
                    mem[t1]? = some m1 →
                      m0.loc = { label := l, offset := o } →
                        m1.loc = { label := l, offset := o + 1 } →
                          WeakMem.hiddenFrom mem { label := l, offset := o } t0 f = false →
                            WeakMem.hiddenFrom mem { label := l, offset := o + 1 } t1 f = false →
                              WeakMem.pairStraddles mem pairs { label := l, offset := o } t0 t1 = false →
                                TermMem.PairOK E c.ty o (TermMem.mw m0) (TermMem.mw m1)
TermStep.SeenOwn : TermSound.TEnv → Prop
  := fun E =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel),
    E.seen l → (∃ c, E.sto l = some c ∧ c.owner = some E.tid) ∨ E.sto l = none ∧ E.box l ≠ none
TermStep.SlotStart : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a =>
  ∃ cl N e us,
    E.sto a.label = some cl ∧
      cl.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
        TermRun.accs e = some us ∧
          0 < TermRun.nWords us ∧ (∀ (u : TermRun.Acc), u ∈ us → u = TermRun.Acc.one) ∧ a.offset % TermRun.nWords us = 0
TermStep.Solid : CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun V =>
  (∀ (a b : CoreLang.Ty), V ≠ a.prod b) ∧
    (∀ (i : CoreLang.Idx) (e : CoreLang.Ty), V ≠ CoreLang.Ty.array i e) ∧ ∀ (t : CoreLang.Ty), V ≠ t.mu
TermStep.TearOK : CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  := fun S =>
  ∀ (E : TermSound.TEnv) (us : List TermRun.Acc) (k : Nat) (ws1 ws2 : List TermRun.Word),
    TermRun.accs S = some us →
      TermTear.Bnd us k →
        TermSound.VR (fun x => 0) (TermSound.CE E) S (TermSound.shp S) ws1 →
          TermSound.VR (fun x => 0) (TermSound.CE E) S (TermSound.shp S) ws2 →
            ws1.length = TermRun.nWords us →
              ws2.length = TermRun.nWords us →
                TermSound.VR (fun x => 0) (TermSound.CE E) S (TermSound.shp S) (TermTear.splice k ws1 ws2)
TermStep.TwoAt : List TermRun.Acc → Nat → Prop
  := fun us o => ∃ i, us[i]? = some TermRun.Acc.two ∧ o = TermRun.nWords (List.take i us)
TermStep.TwoAtT : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → Prop
  := fun T o => ∃ b S us o', TermMem.SegOf T b S ∧ TermRun.accs S = some us ∧ TermStep.TwoAt us o' ∧ o = b + o'
TermStep.TypedAt : TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.Cell → WeakMem.Msg → Prop
  := fun η c m => TermMem.WordOK η c.ty m.loc.offset (TermMem.mw m)
TermStep.VRes : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → TermRun.Word → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E a w R =>
  TermStep.Vis E a.label ∧ TermStep.IniW E a ∧ TermStep.WRes E a w R ∨
    ¬(TermStep.Vis E a.label ∧ TermStep.IniW E a) ∧ R = TermSound.Res.zero
TermStep.ValOfR : TermSound.TEnv → CoreLang.Ty → List TermRun.Word → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun η T ws R => TermSound.VRR (fun x => 0) (TermSound.CE η.unsee) T (TermSound.shp T) ws R ∧ TermSound.Valid T ws
TermStep.Vis : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun E l => ¬TermStep.Priv E l
TermStep.VisU : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a => TermStep.WordUnit E a ∧ TermStep.Vis E a.label
TermStep.WRes : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → TermRun.Word → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E a w R =>
  (∃ n P, TermStep.WordAt E a n P ∧ TermSound.WordHolds P [w.val] ∧ R = TermSound.Res.one (TermSound.Key.rg n)) ∨
    (∀ (n : Nat) (P : CoreLang.Proto), TermStep.WordAt E a n P → ¬TermSound.WordHolds P [w.val]) ∧
      R = TermSound.Res.zero
TermStep.WordAt : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Nat → CoreLang.Proto → Prop
  := fun E a n P =>
  ∃ c,
    E.sto a.label = some c ∧ TermSound.AtOff c.ty a.offset (CoreLang.Ty.word (CoreLang.Idx.lit n) P) ∧ P.ghost = false
TermStep.WordUnit : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → Prop
  := fun E a =>
  ∃ c,
    E.sto a.label = some c ∧
      ((∃ r P, P.ghost = false ∧ TermSound.AtOff c.ty a.offset (CoreLang.Ty.word r P)) ∨
        (∃ r P, TermSound.AtOff c.ty a.offset (CoreLang.Ty.word2 r P)) ∨
          ∃ r P, 1 ≤ a.offset ∧ TermSound.AtOff c.ty (a.offset - 1) (CoreLang.Ty.word2 r P))
TermStep.ZW : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun E l =>
  ∃ c N e us,
    E.sto l = some c ∧
      c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧ TermRun.accs e = some us ∧ TermRun.nWords us = 0
TermStep.ZwL : CoreLang.Ty → List CoreLang.Ty → Prop
  | TermStep.ZwL.zero : ∀ {t : CoreLang.Ty}, TermStep.AtomT t → TermSound.wc t = some 0 → TermStep.ZwL t [t]
  | TermStep.ZwL.pos : ∀ {t : CoreLang.Ty} {n : Nat}, TermStep.AtomT t → TermSound.wc t = some (n + 1) → TermStep.ZwL t []
  | TermStep.ZwL.prod : ∀ {a b : CoreLang.Ty} {A B : List CoreLang.Ty}, TermStep.ZwL a A → TermStep.ZwL b B → TermStep.ZwL (a.prod b) (A ++ B)
  | TermStep.ZwL.mu : ∀ {t : CoreLang.Ty} {L : List CoreLang.Ty}, TermStep.ZwL (CoreTerm.unfold1 t.mu) L → TermStep.ZwL t.mu L
TermStep.ZwZ : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Address → TermSound.Res → Prop
  := fun E a Z =>
  (TermStep.SlotStart E a ∧
      ∃ c N e us Us Rs E0,
        E.sto a.label = some c ∧
          c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
            TermRun.accs e = some us ∧
              TermStep.ZwL e Us ∧
                Rs.length = Us.length ∧
                  Z = TermSound.Res.sum Rs ∧
                    (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box), E0.box l = some B → E.box l = some B) ∧
                      ∀ (i : Nat) (U : CoreLang.Ty) (Ri : TermSound.Res),
                        Us[i]? = some U →
                          Rs[i]? = some Ri →
                            TermSound.VRR (fun x => 0) (TermSound.CE E0.unsee) U (TermSound.shp U) [] Ri) ∨
    ¬TermStep.SlotStart E a ∧ Z = TermSound.Res.zero
TermStep.noOwnR : TermSound.Res → TermSound.Res
  := fun R k =>
  match k with
  | TermSound.Key.own l => 0
  | k => R k
TermStep.pSum : TermRun.St → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res
  := fun s f => TermSound.Res.sum (List.map f s.promises)
TermStep.plantF : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.Ts
  := fun E s l => max s.vs.vWr (TermStep.plantT E s l)
TermStep.plantT : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.Ts
  := fun E s l => List.foldr max 0 (List.filter (fun u => decide (TermStep.HoldW E s l u)) s.promises)
TermStep.ridPair : Nat → Nat → Nat
  := fun t k => 2 ^ t * (2 * k + 1)
TermStep.slotCnt : TermSound.Cell → Nat
  := fun c =>
  match c.ty with
  | CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit Na) τ => Na
  | x => 0
TermStep.winL : WeakMem.Ts → List WeakMem.Ts → List WeakMem.Ts
  := fun wb P => List.filter (fun t => decide (t ≤ wb)) P
TermStep.wr : CoreLang.Proto → Nat → WeakMem.Val → TermSound.Res
  := fun P n v => if TermSound.WordHolds P [v] then TermSound.Res.one (TermSound.Key.rg n) else TermSound.Res.zero
TermStepper.fpProm : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → WeakMem.Ts → Nat → List WeakMem.Ts
  := fun Eg j n i => if i = j then Eg.fpOf i else Eg.fpOf i ++ [n]
TermSys.AllPromLt : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c => ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts), c.ths[i]? = some th → t ∈ th.promises → t < Array.size c.mem
TermSys.BoxTopG : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (B : TermSound.Box),
    Eg.box l = some B →
      TermStep.BoxWords B fun o w =>
        ∃ t, t ≤ B.fr ∧ ¬t ∈ TermCut.allProms c ∧ TermStep.LatestP c.mem [] (fun x => t) { label := l, offset := o } w
TermSys.FpOwnFree : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun Eg c => ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts), c.ths[i]? = some th → t ∈ Eg.fpOf i → ¬t ∈ th.promises
TermSys.FreshU : Nat →
  TermCut.GEnv →
    TermRun.Cfg →
      (Nat → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun N Eg c Tot HP HS cr =>
  ∀ (q j : Nat),
    c.ths.size ≤ q →
      q < N →
        TermSys.FreshX Eg c (TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase N q j) ∧
          TermSys.OwnZ c Tot HP HS cr (TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase N q j)
TermSys.FreshX : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun Eg c x =>
  Eg.sto x = none ∧
    Eg.box x = none ∧
      Eg.ini x = 0 ∧ Eg.pfr x = none ∧ ∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg), c.mem[t]? = some m → m.loc.label ≠ x
TermSys.FrontierLe : TermCut.GEnv → Nat → Prop
  := fun Eg n =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell) (f : WeakMem.Ts), Eg.sto l = some cl → Eg.pfr l = some f → f ≤ n
TermSys.GFreshL : TermCut.GEnv → TermRun.Cfg → TermRun.TTh → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun Eg c th l =>
  Eg.sto l = none ∧
    Eg.box l = none ∧
      Eg.ini l = 0 ∧
        Eg.pfr l = none ∧ ∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg), c.mem[t]? = some m → m.loc.label = l → t ∈ th.promises
TermSys.GStoMg : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell), Eg.sto l = some c → TermTv.mg c.ty = true
TermSys.KeySum : Nat → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res → TermSound.Key → Prop
  := fun N T HS k => (List.map (fun i => T i k) (List.range N)).sum + HS k ≤ 1
TermSys.OwnZ : TermRun.Cfg →
  (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
    (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun c Tot HP HS cr x =>
  (∀ (n : Nat), n < c.ths.size → (Tot n + HP n + TermSys.pSumAt c n (cr n)) (TermSound.Key.own x) = 0) ∧
    HS (TermSound.Key.own x) = 0
TermSys.PromsDisj : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c =>
  ∀ (i j : Nat) (thi thj : TermRun.TTh) (t : WeakMem.Ts),
    i ≠ j → c.ths[i]? = some thi → c.ths[j]? = some thj → t ∈ thi.promises → ¬t ∈ thj.promises
TermSys.SharedTyOK : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell), Eg.Shared l → Eg.sto l = some cl → TermCut.noOR cl.ty = true
TermSys.SysInvW : CoreTerm.Prog →
  Promises.Knobs →
    WeakMem.Arch →
      TermSound.Res →
        (TermSound.Res → Prop) →
          TermRun.Cfg →
            TermCut.GEnv →
              (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                (Nat → TermSound.Res) →
                  TermSound.Res → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  | TermSys.SysInvW.mk : ∀ {p : CoreTerm.Prog} {k : Promises.Knobs} {arch : WeakMem.Arch} {Init : TermSound.Res} {MintOK : TermSound.Res → Prop}
  {c : TermRun.Cfg} {Eg : TermCut.GEnv} {Tot HP : Nat → TermSound.Res} {HS : TermSound.Res}
  {cr db : Nat → WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res},
  (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
      c.ths[i]? = some th →
        TermSys.ThSys p (TermRun.gOf p k arch c i) c.mem c.pairs th Eg (Tot i) (HP i) HS (cr i) (db i)) →
    TermCut.MemHeldG Eg c HS →
      TermCut.GStoClo Eg →
        TermSys.GStoMg Eg →
          TermSys.SharedTyOK Eg →
            TermCut.PromsOK Eg c →
              (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh) (S : WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop),
                  c.ths[i]? = some th → TermStep.FpOK (TermCut.Cut Eg i S) (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th)) →
                (∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh),
                    c.ths[i]? = some th →
                      ∀ (j : Nat), th.nalloc ≤ j → TermSys.GFreshL Eg c th (TermRun.freshLabel c.lbase c.n i j)) →
                  (∃ M,
                      MintOK M ∧
                        (TermSys.thSum c.ths.size fun i => Tot i + TermSys.pSumAt c i (cr i) + HP i) + HS ≤
                          Init + M + TermSys.thSum c.ths.size fun i => TermSys.pSumAt c i (db i)) →
                    (∀ (n : Nat), TermSys.KeySum c.ths.size (fun i => Tot i + HP i) HS (TermSound.Key.rg n)) →
                      (∀ (n : Nat), TermSys.KeySum c.ths.size (fun i => Tot i + HP i) HS (TermSound.Key.wd n)) →
                        (∀ (l : Nat), TermSys.KeySum c.ths.size (fun i => Tot i + HP i) HS (TermSound.Key.own l)) →
                          c.boxFence = true →
                            TermSys.BoxTopG Eg c →
                              TermSys.PromsDisj c →
                                TermSys.FpOwnFree Eg c →
                                  TermSys.AllPromLt c →
                                    TermSys.VWrLe c →
                                      TermSys.FrontierLe Eg (Array.size c.mem) →
                                        (∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), Eg.rini l ≤ Eg.ini l) →
                                          TermCut.GBoxNamed Eg →
                                            TermSys.FreshU c.n Eg c Tot HP HS cr →
                                              TermSys.SysInvW p k arch Init MintOK c Eg Tot HP HS cr db
TermSys.ThSys : CoreTerm.Prog →
  TermRun.G →
    WeakMem.Memory →
      List WeakMem.Ts →
        TermRun.TTh →
          TermCut.GEnv →
            TermSound.Res →
              TermSound.Res → TermSound.Res → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → Prop
  := fun p g mem pairs th Eg Tot HP HS cr db =>
  ∃ S,
    TermSound.ThInvRE p (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) th Tot ∧
      TermSound.ThInvE p (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) th ∧
        TermStep.MemInv g (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) ∧
          TermCut.MemHeldP (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) HP ∧
            TermCut.MemHeldS (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) HS ∧
              TermPlant.PromCrK (TermCut.Cut Eg g.i S) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) cr db
TermSys.VWrLe : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c => ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[i]? = some th → th.vs.vWr ≤ Array.size c.mem
TermSys.pSumAt : TermRun.Cfg → Nat → (WeakMem.Ts → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res
  := fun c i f =>
  match c.ths[i]? with
  | some th => TermStep.pSum (TermRun.stOf c.mem c.pairs th) f
  | none => TermSound.Res.zero
TermSys.thSum : Nat → (Nat → TermSound.Res) → TermSound.Res
  := fun N f => TermSound.Res.sum (List.map f (List.range N))
TermTear.Bnd : List TermRun.Acc → Nat → Prop
  := fun us k => ∃ i, k = TermRun.nWords (List.take i us)
TermTear.splice : Nat → List TermRun.Word → List TermRun.Word → List TermRun.Word
  := fun k ws1 ws2 => List.take k ws1 ++ List.drop k ws2
TermTv.TvOK : CoreTerm.Cx → Prop
  := fun cx =>
  (∀ (e : String × CoreLang.Ty × Bool), e ∈ cx.vars → TermClose.tb (List.length cx.ke) e.snd.fst = true) ∧
    (∀ (tn : CoreTerm.Torn), tn ∈ cx.torn → TermClose.tb (List.length cx.ke) tn.ty = true) ∧
      TermClose.tb (List.length cx.ke) cx.ret = true ∧
        (∀ (e : String × CoreLang.Ty × Bool), e ∈ cx.vars → cx.opaqueOK e.snd.fst = true) ∧
          (∀ (e : String × CoreLang.Ty × Bool), e ∈ cx.vars → TermTv.mg e.snd.fst = true) ∧
            (∀ (tn : CoreTerm.Torn), tn ∈ cx.torn → TermTv.mg tn.ty = true) ∧
              TermTv.mg cx.ret = true ∧ TermTv.noIallV cx.ret = true
TermTv.mg : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  TermTv.mg x =
    match x with
    | t.mu => !CoreLang.Ty.unguarded 0 t && TermTv.mg t
    | a.prod b => TermTv.mg a && TermTv.mg b
    | a.sum b => TermTv.mg a && TermTv.mg b
    | a.code b k => TermTv.mg a && TermTv.mg b
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => TermTv.mg t
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptr μ t => TermTv.mg t
    | CoreLang.Ty.ptrOpt μ t => TermTv.mg t
    | CoreLang.Ty.loc μ t => TermTv.mg t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => TermTv.mg t
    | t.iall => TermTv.mg t
    | t.iex => TermTv.mg t
    | t.refine ψ => TermTv.mg t
    | x => true
TermTv.noIallV : CoreLang.Ty → Bool
  eq_def ∀ (x : CoreLang.Ty),
  TermTv.noIallV x =
    match x with
    | τ.iall => false
    | a.prod b => TermTv.noIallV a && TermTv.noIallV b
    | a.sum b => TermTv.noIallV a && TermTv.noIallV b
    | CoreLang.Ty.array i t => TermTv.noIallV t
    | t.mu => TermTv.noIallV t
    | CoreLang.Ty.tex κ t => TermTv.noIallV t
    | t.iex => TermTv.noIallV t
    | t.refine ψ => TermTv.noIallV t
    | x => true
TermTyped.IniAnch : TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.TEnv → Prop
  := fun E0 E => ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel), E.sto l ≠ none → E.ini l ≤ max (E0.ini l) (E.rini l)
TermTyped.NoHoldPubN : CoreTerm.Prog → Promises.Knobs → WeakMem.Arch → TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.TEnv → Nat → TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun p k arch E0 E1 i c =>
  ∀ (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[i]? = some th → TermTyped.PubWinSAN p (TermRun.gOf p k arch c i) c.mem c.pairs th E0 E1
TermTyped.PlantR : TermSound.TEnv → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → WeakMem.AllocLabel → WeakMem.AllocLabel → Prop
  := fun η mem ps l a =>
  ∃ t,
    t ∈ ps ∧
      ∃ m,
        mem[t]? = some m ∧
          m.loc.label = l ∧
            (∃ ca, η.sto l = some ca ∧ CoreLang.Ty.noOwnIn [] ca.ty = true ∧ TermSound.rgnFree ca.ty = true) ∧
              ∃ a' g,
                m.val = WeakMem.Val.loc a' g ∧
                  a'.label = a ∧ ∃ cl, η.sto a = some cl ∧ cl.owner = some η.tid ∧ η.pfr a = none
TermTyped.PubWinSAN : CoreTerm.Prog → TermRun.G → WeakMem.Memory → List WeakMem.Ts → TermRun.TTh → TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.TEnv → Prop
  := fun p g mem pairs th E0 E1 =>
  ∀ (ρ : Nat → Nat) (η : TermSound.TEnv) (cx : CoreTerm.Cx),
    TermSound.EnvOK p η →
      CoreTyping.Typed p cx th.term →
        TermTv.TvOK cx →
          TermSound.FInv ρ η th.σs (TermSound.Resume p th.stack) cx th.env →
            TermStep.MemInv g (TermSound.CE η) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) →
              TermSound.Ext E1 (TermSound.CE η) →
                TermTyped.IniAnch E1 (TermSound.CE η) →
                  TermTyped.RiniMem E1 (TermSound.CE η) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) →
                    TermTyped.PubWinTN (TermSound.CE η) (TermRun.stOf mem pairs th) th
TermTyped.PubWinTN : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → TermRun.TTh → Prop
  := fun E s th =>
  ∀ (x : String) (a : CoreTerm.At) (e : CoreTerm.Expr) (rt : CoreLang.Ty) (ws : List TermRun.Word)
    (ad : WeakMem.Address),
    th.term = CoreTerm.Expr.let_ x (CoreTerm.Op.publish a) e →
      TermRun.evalAt th.env a = some (rt, ws) →
        List.map (fun x => x.val) ws = [WeakMem.Val.loc ad 0] → ¬E.seen ad.label → TermStep.NoHold E s ad.label
TermTyped.RiniCoh : TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun E s =>
  ∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg) (c : TermSound.Cell) (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc)
    (f : WeakMem.Ts),
    ¬t ∈ s.promises →
      s.mem[t]? = some m →
        E.sto m.loc.label = some c →
          c.owner = some E.tid →
            E.pfr m.loc.label = some f →
              t ≤ f →
                c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
                  TermRun.accs e = some us →
                    E.rc m.loc.label * TermRun.nWords us ≤ m.loc.offset →
                      m.loc.offset < N * TermRun.nWords us → m.val = WeakMem.Val.undef
TermTyped.RiniMem : TermSound.TEnv → TermSound.TEnv → TermRun.St → Prop
  := fun E1 E s =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc) (j : Nat),
    E.sto l = some c →
      c.owner = some E.tid →
        ¬E.seen l →
          c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
            TermRun.accs e = some us →
              0 < TermRun.nWords us →
                j < N →
                  (∀ (o : Nat),
                      j * TermRun.nWords us ≤ o →
                        o < (j + 1) * TermRun.nWords us →
                          ∃ t m,
                            ¬t ∈ s.promises ∧
                              s.mem[t]? = some m ∧
                                m.loc = { label := l, offset := o } ∧
                                  m.val ≠ WeakMem.Val.undef ∧ ∀ (f : WeakMem.Ts), E1.pfr l = some f → t ≤ f) →
                    j < E.rini l
TermUndefLow.OneU : CoreLang.Ty → Nat → Prop
  := fun T o =>
  (∃ L, TermRun.accs T = some L ∧ TermFul.OneAt L o) ∨
    ∃ N e L j o',
      T = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e ∧
        TermRun.accs e = some L ∧ j < N ∧ o = j * TermRun.nWords L + o' ∧ TermFul.OneAt L o'
TermUndefLow.UndefLowG : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.Memory → Prop
  := fun Eg mem =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (cl : TermSound.Cell),
    Eg.sto l = some cl →
      ∀ (o : Nat),
        TermUndefLow.OneU cl.ty o →
          ∀ (t1 t2 : WeakMem.Ts) (m1 m2 : WeakMem.Msg),
            t1 < t2 →
              mem[t1]? = some m1 →
                mem[t2]? = some m2 →
                  m1.loc = { label := l, offset := o } →
                    m2.loc = { label := l, offset := o } → m1.val ≠ WeakMem.Val.undef → m2.val ≠ WeakMem.Val.undef
TermUndefZ.UndefAtG : TermCut.GEnv → WeakMem.Memory → Prop
  := fun Eg mem =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell),
    Eg.sto l = some c →
      ∀ (o n : Nat) (P : CoreLang.Proto),
        TermSound.AtOff c.ty o (CoreLang.Ty.word (CoreLang.Idx.lit n) P) →
          P.ghost = false → ∃ t m, mem[t]? = some m ∧ m.loc = { label := l, offset := o } ∧ m.val = WeakMem.Val.undef
TermViewInv.EnvV : Nat → TermRun.Env → Prop
  := fun n env => ∀ (e : String × CoreLang.Ty × List TermRun.Word), e ∈ env → TermViewInv.WsV n e.snd.snd
TermViewInv.MsgV : WeakMem.Memory → Prop
  := fun mem => ∀ (t : Nat) (m : WeakMem.Msg), mem[t]? = some m → m.view ≤ t
TermViewInv.ThV : Nat → TermRun.TTh → Prop
  := fun n th =>
  TermViewInv.EnvV n th.env ∧
    (∀ (fr : TermRun.Frame), fr ∈ th.stack → TermViewInv.EnvV n fr.env) ∧ TermViewInv.VSB n th.vs
TermViewInv.VSB : Nat → WeakMem.VState → Prop
  := fun n vs => vs.vRd ≤ n ∧ vs.vWr ≤ n ∧ vs.vCtrl ≤ n ∧ vs.vPreBase ≤ n ∧ vs.vRelSt ≤ n
TermViewInv.ViewInv : TermRun.Cfg → Prop
  := fun c =>
  TermViewInv.MsgV c.mem ∧ ∀ (i : Nat) (th : TermRun.TTh), c.ths[i]? = some th → TermViewInv.ThV (Array.size c.mem) th
TermViewInv.WV : Nat → TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun n w => w.view ≤ w.rts ∧ w.rts ≤ n
TermViewInv.WsV : Nat → List TermRun.Word → Prop
  := fun n ws => ∀ (w : TermRun.Word), w ∈ ws → TermViewInv.WV n w
TermZwIni.ZwIniG : TermCut.GEnv → Prop
  := fun Eg =>
  ∀ (l : WeakMem.AllocLabel) (c : TermSound.Cell) (N : Nat) (e : CoreLang.Ty) (us : List TermRun.Acc),
    Eg.pfr l ≠ none →
      ¬Eg.pubd l →
        Eg.sto l = some c →
          c.ty = CoreLang.Ty.array (CoreLang.Idx.lit N) e →
            TermRun.accs e = some us → TermRun.nWords us = 0 → TermStep.InhZ Eg.prog e → N ≤ Eg.ini l
